TL;DR: Proposes Riemannian batch normalization layers for neural networks on complex domains (e.g., Siegel disk), with applications in radar classification, node classification, and action recognition.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: While Riemannian neural networks have proven effective, existing work lacks proper batch normalization layers for complex domains, which are essential building blocks for stable training and improved accuracy.

Method: The paper proposes batch normalization layers for neural networks on complex domains, deriving essential implementation components for complex domains (e.g., Siegel disk) with connections to existing Riemannian BN layers.

Result: Experiments on radar clutter classification, node classification, and action recognition tasks demonstrate the efficacy of the proposed method.

Conclusion: The proposed BN layers effectively enhance training and accuracy for neural networks operating on complex domains like the Siegel disk, with successful applications across multiple domains.

Abstract: Riemannian neural networks have proven effective in solving a variety of machine learning tasks. The key to their success lies in the development of principled Riemannian analogs of fundamental building blocks in deep neural networks (DNNs). Among those, Riemannian batch normalization (BN) layers have shown to enhance training stability and improve accuracy. In this paper, we propose BN layers for neural networks on complex domains. The proposed layers have close connections with existing Riemannian BN layers. We derive essential components for practical implementations of BN layers on some complex domains which are less studied in previous works, e.g., the Siegel disk domain. We conduct experiments on radar clutter classification, node classification, and action recognition demonstrating the efficacy of our method.

</details>


### [99] [Near-optimal and Efficient First-Order Algorithm for Multi-Task Learning with Shared Linear Representation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00473)
*Shihong Ding,Fangyu Du,Cong Fang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了首个具有收敛保证的一阶算法来解决多任务学习的矩阵分解问题。该算法收敛速度为对数O(1)迭代，估计误差达近优O(dk/(TN))，相比现有似然方法提升k倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多任务学习(MTL)通过挖掘多个相关任务间的共享结构取得成功，但基于似然且可高效求解的算法发展不足，主要原因在于矩阵分解引入的非凸结构。

Method: 提出一种一阶算法，联合学习共享表示和任务特定参数。通过分析矩阵分解的非凸景观，利用特定的几何结构实现高效优化。

Result: 算法收敛至对数O(1)迭代量级，估计误差达近优O(dk/(TN))，较现有似然方法提升k倍(d为输入维数，k为表示维数，T为任务数，N为每任务样本数)。

Conclusion: 似然方法的一阶算法可有效求解多任务学习问题，为MTL的优化理论提供坚实保证。

Abstract: Multi-task learning (MTL) has emerged as a pivotal paradigm in machine learning by leveraging shared structures across multiple related tasks. Despite its empirical success, the development of likelihood-based efficiently solvable algorithms--even for shared linear representations--remains largely underdeveloped, primarily due to the non-convex structure intrinsic to matrix factorization. This paper introduces a first-order algorithm that jointly learns a shared representation and task-specific parameters, with guaranteed efficiency. Notably, it converges in $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(1)$ iterations and attains a \emph{near-optimal} estimation error of $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(dk/(TN))$, \emph{improving} over existing likelihood-based methods by a factor of $k$, where $d$, $k$, $T$, $N$ denote input dimension, representation dimension, task count, and samples per task, respectively. Our results justify that likelihood-based first-order methods can efficiently solve the MTL problem.

</details>


### [100] [Trading off rewards and errors in multi-armed bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00488)
*Akram Erraqabi,Alessandro Lazaric,Michal Valko,Emma Brunskill,Yun-En Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: This paper addresses the exploration-exploitation tradeoff in multi-armed bandits by developing an algorithm that balances accurate arm mean estimation with reward accumulation, with theoretical bounds and empirical support.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: In multi-armed bandits, there's a fundamental tension: the most informative arms for identification are typically not the best arms for reward maximization, creating a tradeoff between exploration (identifying arm means accurately) and exploitation (pulling the best arm).

Method: An algorithm with regret guarantees that interpolates between accurate arm mean estimation and reward maximization objectives.

Result: The paper provides both upper and lower bounds on the tradeoff, along with empirical validation of the proposed algorithm.

Conclusion: An algorithm that successfully balances exploration and exploitation in multi-armed bandits, validated through both theoretical analysis and empirical evaluation.

Abstract: In multi-armed bandits, the most-explored arms are the most informative, while reward maximization typically pulls only the best arm. We study the tradeoff between identifying arm means accurately and accumulating reward, and present an algorithm with regret guarantees that interpolates between the two objectives. We provide both upper and lower bounds and validate empirically.

</details>


### [101] [Distance metric learning for conditional anomaly detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00490)
*Michal Valko,Milos Hauskrecht*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于实例的条件异常检测方法，通过度量学习优化距离度量，以准确识别依赖于其他属性值的部分属性异常模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统异常检测方法只能识别整体数据实例中的异常，而无法针对特定属性子集检测条件依赖的异常模式。需要将异常检测扩展为条件异常检测框架，以识别依赖于其他属性值的部分属性异常。

Method: 采用基于实例的检测方法，通过学习距离度量来识别数据集中对检测异常最关键的数据点，从而优化条件异常检测的性能。

Result: 通过度量学习方法学习最优距离度量，能够优化基于实例的条件异常检测方法的性能。

Conclusion: 通过度量学习优化距离度量，能够有效提升条件异常检测的性能，使检测结果更准确地反映条件异常模式。

Abstract: Anomaly detection methods can be very useful in identifying unusual or interesting patterns in data. A recently proposed conditional anomaly detection framework extends anomaly detection to the problem of identifying anomalous patterns on a subset of attributes in the data. The anomaly always depends (is conditioned) on the value of remaining attributes. The work presented in this paper focuses on instance-based methods for detecting conditional anomalies. The methods depend heavily on the distance metric that lets us identify examples in the dataset that are most critical for detecting the anomaly. To optimize the performance of such methods we study and devise a metric learning method that learns the distance metric to reflect best the conditional anomaly pattern.

</details>


### [102] [Beyond Continuity: Simulation-free Reconstruction of Discrete Branching Dynamics from Single-cell Snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00545)
*Junda Ying,Yuxuan Wang,Bowen Yang,Peijie Zhou,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Unbalanced Schrödinger Bridge (USB)框架，用于从单细胞快照数据推断细胞命运轨迹。该方法解决了传统方法在处理细胞增殖/凋亡等非守恒质量动力学及离散跳跃式生死事件方面的不足，同时兼顾随机性和高维组学数据的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非平衡最优传输(OT)方法将质量视为连续流体，在群体水平进行推断，难以捕捉单细胞分辨率下离散、跳跃式的生死事件（如细胞增殖和凋亡），这对于理解谱系分化和命运决定至关重要。

Method: USB框架通过引入模拟自由(simulation-free)的训练目标，实现高效的分支Schrödinger Bridge(BSB)问题求解。理论层面提供可处理的解决方案，为个体细胞同时经历布朗运动和离散生死跳跃的过程提供严格的微观解释；技术层面设计可扩展到高维组学数据的训练目标。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据集上的实验表明，USB不仅在轨迹重建性能上达到或优于确定性基准方法，还能独特地实现单细胞分辨率下对生死动力学的真实离散模拟。

Conclusion: USB为单细胞轨迹推断提供了一个统一框架，有效整合随机性和非平衡效应，同时具备捕捉离散跳跃式动力学的能力，为理解细胞命运决定和谱系分支提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Inferring cellular trajectories from destructive snapshots is complicated by the challenges of stochasticity and non-conservative mass dynamics such as cell proliferation and apoptosis. Existing unbalanced Optimal Transport (OT) methods treat mass as a continuous fluid, performing inference at the population level. However, this macroscopic view often fails to capture the discrete, jump-like nature of birth-death events at single-cell resolution, which is essential for understanding lineage branching and fate decisions. We present Unbalanced Schrödinger Bridge (USB), a simulation-free framework for learning underlying dynamics that effectively integrates both stochastic and unbalanced effects which also models the discrete, jump-like birth-death dynamics at single-cell resolution. Theoretically, USB provides a tractable solution to the Branching Schrödinger Bridge (BSB) problem, offering a rigorous microscopic interpretation where individual cells undergo both Brownian motion and discrete birth-death jumps. Technically, the method implements an efficient solver by introducing a simulation-free training objective that effectively scales to high-dimensional omics data. Empirically, we demonstrate on both simulated and real-world datasets that USB not only achieves trajectory reconstruction performance better than or comparable to deterministic baselines but also uniquely enables realistic discrete simulation of birth-death dynamics at single-cell resolution.

</details>


### [103] [Scaling Federated Linear Contextual Bandits via Sketching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00500)
*Hantao Yang,Hong Xie,Xutong Liu,Defu Lian*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FSCLB通过SVD和双重sketch策略解决联邦上下文线性 bandits 的高计算和通信开销问题，将复杂度从O(d³)/O(d²)降至O(l²d)/O(ld)，实现90%+成本削减且保持接近最优的regret性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦上下文线性 bandits 中，高数据维度导致每轮本地计算需O(d³)行列式运算且通信需上传O(d²)参数，使得现有算法难以扩展到高维场景。

Method: 1) 使用SVD间接计算行列式，避免直接O(d³)行列式计算；2) 引入双重sketch策略同时降低上传和下载成本；3) 用sketch矩阵替代协方差矩阵进行通信决策，保持异步通信条件有效。

Result: 实验验证：合成数据和真实数据集上均实现超过90%的计算和通信成本削减，同时累积奖励仅有微小损失。

Conclusion: FSCLB通过将计算复杂度从O(d³)降低到O(l²d)，通信复杂度从O(d²)降低到O(ld)，实现了超过90%的成本削减，同时仅牺牲可忽略的累积奖励。理论证明当l超过协方差矩阵秩时， regret bound简化为Õ(√(ldT))，达到无sketch情况下的最优regret。

Abstract: In federated contextual linear bandits, high data dimensionality incurs prohibitive computation and communication costs: local agents perform $O(d^3)$-time determinant computation and upload $O(d^2)$ parameters, making existing algorithms unscalable, where $d$ is the dimension of data. To relieve these scaling bottlenecks, this paper proposes Federated Sketch Contextual Linear Bandits (FSCLB). On the computation side, FSCLB uses SVD to indirectly obtain the determinant required for communication, eliminating the prohibitive cost of direct determinant calculation and cutting complexity from $O(d^3)$ to $O(l^2d)$ per round, where $l< d$ is the sketch size. On the communication side, FSCLB introduces a double-sketch strategy that reduces both upload and download costs from $O(d^2)$ to $O(ld)$. Naively involving sketch update into federated contextual linear bandits can destroy the local increment and invalidate the asynchronous communication condition; FSCLB solves this by replacing the covariance matrix with the sketch matrix when deciding whether to communicate. Theoretically, FSCLB achieves a regret bound of $\widetilde{O} ((\sqrt{d}+\sqrt{M\varepsilon_l})\sqrt{lT})$, where $\varepsilon_l$ is the upper bounded by the spectral tail of the covariance matrix; when $l$ exceeds the rank of the covariance matrix, the bound simplifies to $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{ldT})$, matching the optimal no-sketch regret. Experiments on both synthetic and real-world datasets show that FSCLB significantly reduces computational and communication costs by over 90 \% while sacrificing only a negligible amount of cumulative reward.

</details>


### [104] [LambdaRankIC: Directly Optimizing Rank IC for Financial Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00501)
*Yan Lin,Yihong Su,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LambdaRankIC是一种直接优化金融预测排序指标Rank IC的新型学习排序方法，通过推导pairwise rank swaps的闭式lambda梯度解决排序算子不可微问题，实现在XGBoost中的高效梯度优化，理论可证明优化Rank IC上界，实证在仿真和真实市场数据中均优于传统回归和NDCG排序方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融预测模型多采用回归损失或与传统排序目标训练，但这些损失函数与实际评估指标Rank IC（预测与真实收益的Spearman秩相关）存在不一致性，导致模型优化方向偏离实际评价标准。

Method: 提出LambdaRankIC方法：1)推导 pairwise rank swaps 的闭式 lambda 梯度表达式以绕过排序算子的不可微问题；2)在XGBoost中实现为自定义目标函数；3)理论证明该方法优化Rank IC的上界。

Result: 仿真实验中，LambdaRankIC在无噪声环境下准确恢复真实排名结构，在低信噪比和重尾噪声场景下持续优于回归方法和NDCG导向排序方法；真实市场数据实证中，LambdaRankIC在Rank IC、ICIR、月收益、夏普比率等金融常用指标上均取得最佳样本外表现。

Conclusion: 直接优化Rank IC在以全序排名质量为主要目标的金融预测任务中可带来实质性提升。LambdaRankIC为金融机器学习提供了一种更符合评价指标的训练范式。

Abstract: In financial predictions, the performance of machine learning models is often assessed by Rank IC, which is the Spearman rank correlation between the model predictions and the realized asset returns. Despite its wide adoption, most existing models are trained using regression losses or ranking objectives that may not align with Rank IC. We propose LambdaRankIC, a novel learning-to-rank approach that directly optimizes Rank IC. We circumvent the non-differentiability of the ranking operator by deriving the closed-form expression for the lambda gradients induced by the pairwise rank swaps, which enables efficient gradient-based optimization within the LambdaRank framework. We implement LambdaRankIC as a custom objective in XGBoost. Theoretically, we show that our approach optimizes an upper bound on Rank IC. We evaluate the proposed approach on both simulated and real-world financial data. In simulation studies, LambdaRankIC accurately recovers the true ranking structure in noiseless settings and consistently outperforms regression-based and NDCG-oriented ranking methods under low signal-to-noise ratios and heavy-tailed noise regimes. In empirical experiments using real market data, LambdaRankIC achieves the best out-of-sample performance on evaluation metrics commonly used in finance, including Rank IC, ICIR, monthly return, and Sharpe ratio. These results show that directly optimizing Rank IC can yield substantial improvements over conventional learning objectives in financial predictions when the full-order ranking quality is the primary goal.

</details>


### [105] [A Comparative Study of QSPR Methods on a Unique Multitask PAMPA dataset](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00508)
*Andrs Formanek,Anna Vincze,Richrd Bicsak,Yves Moreau,Gyorgy T. Balogh,Adam Arany*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究构建了包含143个药物分子的多任务数据集，系统评估了从线性回归到Transformer等多种模型在预测被动膜通透性方面的表现。研究发现，对于样本量有限的渗透性研究，专家设计的物化性质描述符优于深度学习方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前缺乏针对多种器官特异性PAMPA膜的系统性建模研究，且机器学习方法的可解释性与预测性能之间的权衡尚未得到充分探讨。

Method: 构建多任务数据集（含143个药物分子×6种模型膜），比较线性回归、随机森林、深度学习及Transformer等模型，使用体外PAMPA实验数据进行验证。

Result: 专家设计的物化性质描述符在有限样本场景下优于深度学习表示；多膜联合建模提供了膜特异性渗透特征的新见解。

Conclusion: 对于小样本渗透性研究，传统专家特征优于端到端深度学习；多器官PAMPA联合建模具有重要价值。

Abstract: We present a unique, multitask dataset comprising 143 drug and drug candidate molecules, each evaluated on in vitro, parallel artificial-membrane permeability assays (PAMPA) using six different model membranes. Using this resource, we systematically assess the effectiveness of various molecular descriptors and regression models in predicting passive membrane permeability. The studied models range from simple linear regression to a modern pre-trained transformer architecture. Particular attention is given to the trade-off between predictive performance and model interpretability, highlighting the challenges introduced by machine learning approaches. To our knowledge, this is the most comprehensive study on simultaneous modeling of multiple organ-specific PAMPA membranes to date, offering novel insights into membrane-specific permeability profiles.
  We found that expert-designed physico-chemical property descriptors are more fitting for a limited sample size permeabilty study than deep learning based representations.

</details>


### [106] [Fairness of Classifiers in the Presence of Constraints between Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00592)
*Martin C. Cooper,Imane Bousdira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出当特征间存在约束时，传统的公平性定义可能失效。为此，文章提出基于"公平解释"（fair explanation）的公平性定义——即一个决策的充分必要条件（prime-implicant）中不包含受保护特征。论文证明：忽略约束可能完全改变公平性判断，即使受保护与不受保护特征间无直接约束。进一步，论文定义了分类器的三种公平性并研究其计算复杂性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统公平性定义假设决策不应依赖受保护特征（如性别），但当特征间存在约束时，这种依赖可能被掩盖，导致公平性判断失效。例如，受保护特征可能通过与其他特征的约束关系间接影响决策。

Method: 1. 提出"公平解释"概念：决策的prime-implicant理由中不包含任何受保护特征（约束条件纳入prime-implicant定义）；2. 基于此提出分类器的三种公平性定义：(1) 所有决策仅存在公平解释；(2) 每个决策至少有一个公平解释；(3) 改变受保护特征不改变结果；3. 分析三种定义的关系并研究其计算复杂性。

Result: 核心发现：即使受保护特征与不受保护特征间无直接约束，忽略约束也可能完全改变决策的公平性判断。论文建立了三种公平性定义间的关系，并证明了对分类器公平性检验的计算复杂性。

Conclusion: 论文通过引入公平解释的概念和三种公平性定义，为约束存在下的机器学习公平性提供了理论基础，并揭示了忽视约束的危害。该工作对理解约束如何影响公平性判断具有重要意义。

Abstract: In Machine Learning, an accepted definition of fairness of a decision taken by a classifier is that it should not depend on protected features, such as gender. Unfortunately, when constraints exist between features, such dependencies can be obscured by the constraints.
  To avoid this problem, we propose that a decision be considered fair if it has a fair explanation. We define a fair explanation as a prime-implicant reason for the decision that does not contain any protected feature (where the constraints are taken into account in the definition of prime-implicant). Surprisingly, ignoring constraints can completely change the fairness of a decision (according to this definition) even in the absence of constraints between protected and unprotected features.
  Three possible definitions of fairness of a classifier are that for all its decisions (1) there are only fair explanations, (2) there is at least one fair explanation, or (3) changing protected features does not change the outcome. We identify the relationships between these different definitions of fairness and study the computational complexity of testing fairness of classifiers.

</details>


### [107] [Learning Multimodal Energy-Based Model with Multimodal Variational Auto-Encoder via MCMC Revision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00644)
*Jiali Cui,Zhiqiang Lao,Heather Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Proposes an interleaved learning framework for multimodal EBMs that combines a shared latent generator, joint inference model, and EBM sampling, where MCMC refinement in both data and latent spaces addresses mixing issues and enables coherent multimodal sample generation, demonstrating superior performance over baselines.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Multimodal EBMs trained by maximum likelihood face poor mixing in MCMC sampling, failing to capture coherent inter-modal relationships. Multimodal VAEs address this by introducing shared latent generators and joint inference, but their unimodal Gaussian/Laplace assumptions limit approximation of complex multimodal structures.

Method: Presents an interleaved learning framework that combines a shared latent generator with informative latent initialization, a joint inference model, and energy-based models (EBMs), where MCMC sampling refines samples in both data and latent spaces to enable effective EBM sampling and learning.

Result: The proposed framework enables generation of realistic and coherent multimodal samples, with superior performance in multimodal synthesis quality and coherence compared to baselines. Ablation studies validate its effectiveness and scalability.

Conclusion: Proposes a joint learning framework that interweaves MLE updates with MCMC refinements, demonstrating superior performance in multimodal synthesis quality and coherence compared to various baselines.

Abstract: Energy-based models (EBMs) are a flexible class of deep generative models and are well-suited to capture complex dependencies in multimodal data. However, learning multimodal EBM by maximum likelihood requires Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) sampling in the joint data space, where noise-initialized Langevin dynamics often mixes poorly and fails to discover coherent inter-modal relationships. Multimodal VAEs have made progress in capturing such inter-modal dependencies by introducing a shared latent generator and a joint inference model. However, both the shared latent generator and joint inference model are parameterized as unimodal Gaussian (or Laplace), which severely limits their ability to approximate the complex structure induced by multimodal data. In this work, we study the learning problem of the multimodal EBM, shared latent generator, and joint inference model. We present a learning framework that effectively interweaves their MLE updates with corresponding MCMC refinements in both the data and latent spaces. Specifically, the generator is learned to produce coherent multimodal samples that serve as strong initial states for EBM sampling, while the inference model is learned to provide informative latent initializations for generator posterior sampling. Together, these two models serve as complementary models that enable effective EBM sampling and learning, yielding realistic and coherent multimodal EBM samples. Extensive experiments demonstrate superior performance for multimodal synthesis quality and coherence compared to various baselines. We conduct various analyses and ablation studies to validate the effectiveness and scalability of the proposed multimodal framework.

</details>


### [108] [AdaMeZO: Adam-style Zeroth-Order Optimizer for LLM Fine-tuning Without Maintaining the Moments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00650)
*Zhijie Cai,Haolong Chen,Guangxu Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出AdaMeZO，一种在零阶梯优化框架下融入Adam风格估计的内存高效优化器，在保持低内存占用的同时显著提升收敛速度，实验证明其性能超越MeZO且前向传播次数减少70%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的零阶梯优化器MeZO仅依赖前向传播来微调LLM，可大幅降低GPU内存需求，但收敛速度较慢；标准Adam优化器虽能更好地探索损失景观以加速收敛，但会占用大量内存，从而抵消了MeZO的内存优势。

Method: AdaMeZO通过在内存中不维护一阶和二阶矩估计的情况下，利用Adam风格的一阶和二阶矩估计来实现零阶优化，从而在不增加内存开销的情况下探索损失景观。

Result: AdaMeZO在性能上优于MeZO，同时所需的前向传播次数减少高达70%；轨迹可视化证实了AdaMeZO能够自适应适应多样化的损失景观。

Conclusion: AdaMeZO成功地将Adam风格的优化思想应用于零阶梯优化器，在保持低内存占用的同时显著提升了收敛速度，证明了其在适应多样化损失景观方面的能力。

Abstract: Fine-tuning LLMs is necessary for various dedicated downstream tasks, but classic backpropagation-based fine-tuning methods require substantial GPU memory. To this end, a recent work, MeZO, which relies solely on forward passes to fine-tune LLMs, significantly reduces GPU requirements at the cost of slower convergence due to its indifference to loss landscapes. Standard solutions, such as Adam, explore loss landscapes by estimating the first- and second-order moments and storing them in memory to guide the model's movement through dimensions with lower curvature and vice versa. However, directly applying Adam negates MeZO's advantage as it will triple the memory requirement. In light of this, we propose AdaMeZO, a zeroth-order optimizer that leverages Adam-style first- and second-moment estimates without maintaining them in memory. We present a theoretical analysis of AdaMeZO, corroborated by extensive experiments demonstrating AdaMeZO's performance, showing that AdaMeZO can outperform MeZO while requiring up to $70\%$ fewer forward passes. Trajectory visualizations affirm AdaMeZO's ability to adapt to diverse loss landscapes.

</details>


### [109] [Stable-GFlowNet: Toward Diverse and Robust LLM Red-Teaming via Contrastive Trajectory Balance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00553)
*Minchan Kwon,Sunghyun Baek,Minseo Kim,Jaemyung Yu,Dongyoon Han,Junmo Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Stable-GFN (S-GFN)，针对GFN在LLM红队测试中的训练不稳定和模式坍塌问题，通过消除配分函数估计、鲁棒掩码和流畅度稳定器三项改进，显著提升了攻击效果和多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM红队测试对于保障模型安全至关重要，但现有方法难以同时实现有效且多样的攻击；GFN作为有前景的方法存在训练不稳定和模式坍塌问题，红队测试中不稳定的奖励信号加剧了这一问题。

Method: 通过成对比较消除GFN中的配分函数Z估计，采用鲁棒掩码方法处理奖励噪声，并引入流畅度稳定器防止模型陷入产生乱码的局部最优。

Result: S-GFN在保持GFN最优策略的同时实现了更稳定的训练，在多种设置下均展现出更优的攻击性能和多样性。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明S-GFN在攻击性能和多样性方面均优于对比方法，验证了该方法在LLM红队测试中的有效性和稳定性。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) Red-Teaming, which proactively identifies vulnerabilities of LLMs, is an essential process for ensuring safety. Finding effective and diverse attacks in red-teaming is important, but achieving both is challenging. Generative Flow Networks (GFNs) that perform distribution matching are a promising methods, but they are notorious for training instability and mode collapse. In particular, unstable rewards in red-teaming accelerate mode collapse. We propose Stable-GFN (S-GFN), which eliminates partition function $Z$ estimation in GFN and reduces training instability. S-GFN avoids Z-estimation through pairwise comparisons and employs a robust masking methodology against noisy rewards. Additionally, we propose a fluency stabilizer to prevent the model from getting stuck in local optima that produce gibberish. S-GFN provides more stable training while maintaining the optimal policy of GFN. We demonstrate the overwhelming attack performance and diversity of S-GFN across various settings.

</details>


### [110] [Reinforcement Learning with Markov Risk Measures and Multipattern Risk Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00654)
*Andrzej Ruszczynski,Tiangang Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对风险厌恶型有限时域MDP，提出mini-batch Markov相干风险度量与多模式风险厌恶问题框架。基于特征基Q-learning方法，在多模式Q因子近似下证明最优O(H²N^H √K)遗憾界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统风险敏感型MDP方法在大规模问题中面临计算复杂度挑战，需要引入新的风险度量框架以支持高效的近似学习。

Method: 提出mini-batch风险度量与多模式Q因子近似方法，设计特征基Q-learning算法，实现向后策略评估的经济版本。

Result: 高概率遗憾界为O(H²N^H √K)，其中H为时域、N为mini-batch大小、K为episode数量。

Conclusion: 理论结果在随机分配问题和短视界多臂老虎机问题上得到验证，提出的经济版Q-learning方法简化了策略评估步骤。

Abstract: For a risk-averse finite-horizon Markov Decision Problem, we introduce a special class of Markov coherent risk measures, called mini-batch measures. We also define the class of multipattern risk-averse problems that generalizes the class of linear systems. We use both concepts in a feature-based $Q$-learning method with multipattern $Q$-factor approximation and we prove a high-probability regret bound of $\mathcal{O}\big(H^2 N^H \sqrt{ K}\big)$, where $H$ is the horizon, $N$ is the mini-batch size, and $K$ is the number of episodes. We also propose an economical version of the $Q$-learning method that streamlines the policy evaluation (backward) step. The theoretical results are illustrated on a stochastic assignment problem and a short-horizon multi-armed bandit problem.

</details>


### [111] [Affinity Is Not Enough: Recovering the Free Energy Principle in Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00604)
*Man Yung Wong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Sparse MoE routing fails at domain transitions, where the current token belongs to one distribution and the next to another. In a controlled experiment (4 experts, 5 seeds), standard affinity routing assigns only 0.006 +/- 0.001 probability to the correct expert at the transition. Three lightweight gate modifications raise this to 0.748 +/- 0.002 (124x), cutting experts needed for 99% coverage from infeasible to a small constant: temporal memory (beta), a per-expert LIF membrane potential accumulating routing context across tokens; precision-weighted gating (Pi), a per-expert inverse variance of recent prediction error, yielding 31x contrast between reliable and unreliable experts; and anticipatory routing, a next-state predictor conditioned on the beta-accumulated hidden state. The mechanisms draw from Friston's Free Energy Principle and use LIF dynamics from spiking neural networks. An ablation across all 2^3 subsets reveals a super-additive beta x Ant interaction: anticipation alone gives nothing (+0.000 +/- 0.001); beta alone gives modest gain (+0.295 +/- 0.013); combined they close 75% of the oracle gap (+0.741 +/- 0.002, exceeding the sum by +0.446 +/- 0.014). This is structural: a stateless predictor cannot detect approaching transitions because pre-transition tokens are distributionally identical to within-domain tokens. In a character-level MoE LM (5 seeds), beta-routing reduces transition-step BPC from 6.56 +/- 0.01 (Standard) to 4.01 +/- 0.15 (beta-MoE); the beta + Ant gate places 0.86 +/- 0.02 probability on the correct domain expert before that domain appears in input, vs 0.42 +/- 0.12 for Standard MoE. Reference implementations (~200 lines each): https://github.com/russellwmy/affinity-is-not-enough

</details>


### [112] [Decouple before Integration: Test-time Synthesis of SFT and RLVR Task Vectors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00610)
*Chaohao Yuan,Chenghao Xiao,Yu Rong,Hong Cheng,Long-Kai Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: SFT and RLVR represent two fundamental yet distinct paradigms for LLM post-training, each excelling in distinct dimensions. SFT expands knowledge breadth while RLVR enhances reasoning depth. Yet integrating these complementary strengths remains a formidable challenge. Sequential training can cause catastrophic forgetting, and joint optimization often suffers from severe gradient conflicts. We analyze SFT and RLVR through the lens of task vectors and reveal three structural properties behind these failures: a 30* magnitude disparity, 45* sign interference, and heterogeneous module-wise update distributions. These findings show SFT and RLVR are difficult to integrate directly, but they also suggest that the two paradigms modify partly complementary components of the model. Motivated by these observations, we propose Decoupled Test-time Synthesis (DoTS), a post-hoc framework allows SFT and RLVR checkpoints to be trained independently and synthesizes their capabilities only at inference time via task vector arithmetic, without updating model parameters. To reduce interference, DOTS applies selective sparsification with norm-preserving rescaling. It then uses Bayesian optimization on a small set of unlabeled queries to search for combination coefficients on the Pareto frontier of consistency and perplexity. Empirically, \ours matches or exceeds the performance of training-based SFT--RLVR integration methods across multiple mathematical reasoning benchmarks, incurring only $\sim$3\% of the computational cost. When applied to stronger post-trained checkpoints, DOTS surpasses SOTA models and generalizes to out-of-domain benchmarks without re-tuning. Code is available at https://github.com/chaohaoyuan/DoTS.

</details>


### [113] [Class Angular Distortion Index for Dimensionality Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00637)
*Kaviru Gunaratne,Stephen Kobourov,Jacob Miller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Class Angular Distortion Index (CADI)，一种基于点三元组内角的聚类保真度度量指标，用于评估降维可视化中聚类排列的忠实度。该指标可微分，支持优化，并能在现有指标失效的情况下提供可解释的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing dimensionality reduction techniques have a tension between preserving global vs. local structures; even when clusters appear distinct, their relative arrangement may be arbitrary or misleading (t-SNE, UMAP); current cluster quality metrics either only measure separability or assume spherical clusters in original space

Method: Uses internal angles among point triples to determine the faithfulness of cluster organization; differentiable design enables gradient-based optimization

Result: Shows cases on both real and synthetic data where existing cluster metrics fail, while CADI provides interpretable results; demonstrates successful optimization using CADI as an objective

Conclusion: CADI-based DR technique demonstrates that the metric can be used as an optimization objective, enabling the creation of projections that better preserve angular relationships between clusters.

Abstract: Dimensionality reduction (DR) techniques are often characterized by whether they preserve global, high-level structures in the data or local, neighborhood structures. This distinction matters in visualization: global methods can obscure clusters while local methods can over-emphasize them. Yet, even when clusters appear distinct, their relative arrangement in the projection may be arbitrary or misleading, a common issue in techniques such as t-SNE and UMAP. Existing cluster quality metrics either only measure cluster separability or assume spherical, globular clusters in the original space. We introduce the Class Angular Distortion Index (CADI), a metric that uses internal angles among point triples to determine the faithfulness of cluster organization in a projection. We show cases on both real and synthetic data where existing cluster metrics fail, but CADI provides an interpretable result. Since it relies on computing angles, CADI is also differentiable, enabling optimization. We demonstrate this with a CADI-based DR technique.

</details>


### [114] [From Prediction to Practice: A Task-Aware Evaluation Framework for Blood Glucose Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00645)
*Alireza Namazi,Heman Shakeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对血糖预测提出了任务感知评估框架，揭示标准预测指标与下游任务实用性之间存在显著差距，为临床决策支持系统的评估提供了更严格的评估范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床时间序列预测的标准聚合指标可能掩盖模型在实际任务中的效用。在安全关键场景中，低平均误差可能与高风险区域中的危险失败并存。现有标准预测评估无法检验模型是否具备推理干预措施效果的能力，而这正是支持胰岛素剂量决策的关键需求。

Method: 提出了一个任务感知评估框架，包含两个分支：（1）基于三个临床队列数据，使用事件级召回率和每患者日假警报数评估低血糖预警；（2）使用FDA认可的UVA/Padova模拟器，在反事实场景中评估预测器预测胰岛素干预效果的能力。同时引入临床动机成本函数评估胰岛素剂量选择质量。

Result: 尽管在完整测试集上召回率超过0.9的模型在整体表现可接受，但在胰岛素作用期间（post-bolus切片）表现严重下降。在模拟器评估中，那些在真实数据预测中表现良好的模型常常无法正确预测干预效果的方向、幅度或排序，在临床成本函数下选择较差的胰岛素剂量。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了预测准确性与任务相关实用性之间存在一致差距。团队发布了基准测试套件、标准化预处理流程和基于模拟器的干预数据集作为可重复使用工具包。

Abstract: Clinical time-series forecasting is increasingly studied for decision support, yet standard aggregate metrics can obscure whether a model is actually useful for the task it is meant to serve. In safety-critical settings, low average error can coexist with dangerous failures in exactly the high-risk regimes that matter most. We present a task-aware evaluation framework for blood glucose forecasting built around two downstream uses: hypoglycemia early warning and insulin dosing decision support. For early warning, we evaluate on real data from three clinical cohorts using event-level recall and false alarms per patient-day, metrics that reflect operational alarm burden rather than aggregate accuracy. We show that models appearing acceptable overall, with recall above 0.9 on the full test set, can fail badly in the post-bolus slice, where insulin-on-board is elevated and missed warnings carry the greatest clinical consequences. Standard forecasting evaluation, however, does not test whether a model can reason about the effects of actions, a requirement for supporting insulin dosing decisions. We therefore add a second, interventional arm using the FDA-accepted UVA/Padova simulator, where we evaluate whether forecasters can predict glucose responses to altered insulin plans in paired factual/counterfactual scenarios. We show that models that look strong on real-data forecasting often fail to predict the direction, magnitude, or ranking of intervention effects, and choose poor insulin doses when evaluated under a clinically motivated cost. Taken together, the two arms reveal a consistent gap between forecasting accuracy and task-relevant usefulness. We release the benchmark, the standardized preprocessing pipeline for public cohorts, and the simulator-based interventional dataset as a reproducible toolkit.

</details>


### [115] [PEACE: Cross-modal Enhanced Pediatric-Adult ECG Alignment for Robust Pediatric Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00647)
*Xinran Liu,Yuwen Li,Hongxiang Gao,Heyang Xu,Jianqing Li,Zongmin Wang,Chengyu Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PEACE是一个用于成人到儿科ECG迁移的结构化跨模态对齐框架，通过临床语义分解和课程门控优化等技术，在儿科ECG诊断中实现了有效的低资源迁移学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 成人ECG数据训练的模型在应用到儿科人群时面临显著的跨人群分布不匹配问题，而儿科标注数据本身又十分稀缺，这一矛盾严重制约了儿科ECG自动诊断的发展。

Method: PEACE框架包含三个核心模块：①三轴临床语义分解，②标签查询特征提取，③课程门控优化。通过这三者实现成人ECG表示与儿科诊断目标的对齐。训练中利用Gemini生成标签条件语义描述作为辅助监督，推理阶段仅使用ECG数据。

Result: 在ZZU-pECG数据集上，PEACE分别达到零样本59.39%、50样本79.03%、全微调90.89%的AUC；在PTB-XL共享标签空间上达到96.65%的AUC。

Conclusion: PEACE在成人到儿科ECG迁移中展现出显著潜力，尤其在低资源场景下表现突出，但仍需前瞻性临床验证和更明确的年龄感知建模才能用于实际临床场景。

Abstract: Automated pediatric electrocardiogram (ECG) diagnosis remains challenging because models trained predominantly on adult data suffer from substantial cross-population mismatch, while pediatric labels are often scarce. We present PEACE (Pediatric-Adult ECG Alignment via Cross-modal Enhancement), a structured cross-modal alignment framework for adult-to-pediatric ECG transfer. PEACE integrates tri-axial clinical semantic decomposition, label-query feature extraction, and curriculum-gated optimization to align transferable adult ECG representations with pediatric diagnostic targets. Since ZZU-pECG provides no paired clinical reports, we generate label-conditioned semantic descriptors using Gemini with concise clinical prompts and use them only as auxiliary training supervision; inference remains ECG-only. On ZZU-pECG, PEACE achieves 59.39%, 79.03%, and 90.89% AUC under zero-shot, 50-shot, and full fine-tuning settings, respectively, and reaches 96.65% AUC on the shared PTB-XL label space. These results suggest that structured clinical semantic supervision can improve low-resource adult-to-pediatric ECG transfer, while prospective clinical validation and more explicit age-aware modeling remain necessary before real-world deployment.

</details>


### [116] [Evaluating the Architectural Reasoning Capabilities of LLM Provers via the Obfuscated Natural Number Game](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00677)
*Lixing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出"架构推理"作为衡量AI真正数学推理能力的核心指标，通过混淆的自然数游戏基准测试发现：推理专用模型能在无语义线索环境下保持性能，而通用模型则明显退化，证明该基准可有效区分真推理与模式匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型在形式数学基准（如MiniF2F）上取得了成功，但尚不清楚这些结果是基于真正的逻辑推理还是对预训练数据的语义模式匹配。为实现未来自动定理发现AI，需要一种方法来区分这两种能力。

Method: 通过重命名Lean 4中自然数游戏的标识符，创建了"Obfuscated Natural Number Game"——一个零知识、封闭的测试环境。该基准专门用于评估模型在缺乏语义线索的情况下，仅使用局部公理和定义合成形式证明的能力。

Result: 实验发现存在普遍的"延迟税"现象——混淆后的推理时间显著增加。更重要的是，模型鲁棒性呈现明显分化：通用模型（Claude-Sonnet-4.5、GPT-4o）性能下降，而推理模型（DeepSeek-R1、GPT-5、DeepSeek-Prover-V2）在缺乏语义线索的情况下仍能保持同等准确率。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了"架构推理"（Architectural Reasoning）作为评估AI真正数学推理能力的关键指标，并提供了可量化的测试方法。通过混淆自然数游戏创建的零知识封闭环境，能够有效区分模型是基于语义模式匹配还是真正的逻辑推理能力工作。

Abstract: While Large Language Models have achieved notable success on formal mathematics benchmarks such as MiniF2F, it remains unclear whether these results stem from genuine logical reasoning or semantic pattern matching against pre-training data. This paper identifies Architectural Reasoning: the ability to synthesize formal proofs using exclusively local axioms and definitions within an alien math domain, as the necessary ability for future automated theorem discovery AI. We use the Obfuscated Natural Number Game, a benchmark to evaluate Architectural Reasoning. By renaming identifiers in the Natural Number Game in Lean 4, we created a zero-knowledge, closed environment. We evaluate state-of-the-art models, finding a universal latency tax where obfuscation increases inference time. The results also reveal a divergence in robustness: while general models (Claude-Sonnet-4.5, GPT-4o) suffer performance degradation, reasoning models (DeepSeek-R1, GPT-5, DeepSeek-Prover-V2) maintain the same accuracy despite the absence of semantic cues. These findings provide a quantitative metric for assessing the true capacity for mathematical reasoning.

</details>


### [117] [Deep Kernel Learning for Stratifying Glaucoma Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00708)
*Bruce Rushing,Angela Danquah,Alireza Namazi,Arjun Dirghangi,Heman Shakeri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于深度核学习(DKL)结合高斯过程(GP)和临床BERT嵌入的新架构,用于从多模态电子健康记录(EHR)中识别青光眼患者的疾病进展风险亚组,实现风险分层而非单纯依赖当前疾病状态的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 慢性疾病如青光眼的患者风险分层是重大临床挑战。临床医生需要从稀疏且不规则采样的电子健康记录中识别进展风险高的患者,但传统方法难以捕捉疾病动态演变过程。

Method: 提出一种新型深度核学习(DKL)架构:使用基于Transformer的特征提取器处理临床BERT嵌入,定义高斯过程(GP)的核函数,建模青光眼患者从多模态EHR数据中的疾病轨迹。

Result: 成功识别出三个临床特征显著不同的患者亚组。关键发现:模型学会了将疾病进展与当前严重程度解耦,识别出一个高风险组——尽管平均视敏度优于另一组,但呈现恶化轨迹。这表明模型学习的是识别进展风险,而非仅评估当前疾病状态。

Conclusion: 该方法能够基于风险轨迹对患者进行分层,为临床决策支持提供有力工具,可实现高危个体的针对性干预,改善青光眼护理管理。

Abstract: Effectively stratifying patient risk in chronic diseases like glaucoma is a major clinical challenge. Clinicians need tools to identify patients at high risk of progression from sparse and irregularly-sampled electronic health records (EHRs). We propose a novel deep kernel learning (DKL) architecture that leverages a Gaussian Process (GP) backend. The GP's kernel is defined by a transformer-based feature extractor applied to clinical-BERT embeddings to model glaucoma patient trajectories from multimodal EHR data. Our method successfully identifies three clinically distinct patient subgroups. Crucially, the model learns to decouple disease progression from current severity, identifying a high-risk group with a worsening trajectory despite having better average visual acuity than a second, stably poor group. This reveals that the model learns to identify progression risk rather than just the current disease state. This ability to stratify patients based on their risk trajectory progression offers a powerful tool for clinical decision support, enabling targeted interventions for high-risk individuals and improving the management of glaucoma care.

</details>


### [118] [Aitchison Embeddings for Learning Compositional Graph Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00716)
*Nikolaos Nakis,Chrysoula Kosma,Panagiotis Promponas,Michail Chatzianastasis,Giannis Nikolentzos*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Representation learning is central to graph machine learning, powering tasks such as link prediction and node classification. However, most graph embeddings are hard to interpret, offering limited insight into how learned features relate to graph structure. Many networks naturally admit a role-mixture view, where nodes are best described as mixtures over latent archetypal factors. Motivated by this structure, we propose a compositional graph embedding framework grounded in Aitchison geometry, the canonical geometry for comparing mixtures. Nodes are represented as simplex-valued compositions and embedded via isometric log-ratio (ILR) coordinates, which preserve Aitchison distances while enabling unconstrained optimization in Euclidean space. This yields intrinsically interpretable embeddings whose geometry reflects relative trade-offs among archetypes and supports coherent behavior under component restriction; we consider both fixed and learnable ILR bases. Across node classification and link prediction, our method achieves competitive performance with strong baselines while providing explainability by construction rather than post-hoc. Finally, subcompositional coherence enables principled component restriction: removing and renormalizing subsets preserves a well-defined geometry, which we exploit via subcompositional dimensionality removal to probe how archetype groups influence representations and predictions.

</details>


### [119] [Temporal Data Requirement for Predicting Unplanned Hospital Readmissions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00738)
*Ramin Mohammadi,Vahab vahdat,Sarthak Jain,Amir T. Namin,Ramya Palacholla,Sagar Kamarthi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: The study challenges the assumption that more historical EHR data yields better machine learning predictions by demonstrating modality-specific optimal time windows: clinical notes perform best with 3-6 month observation windows, whereas structured data plateaus at 12 months for predicting 30-day readmission after arthroplasty.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: A critical challenge in building predictive models using EHR data is determining the optimal historical data time window to maximize model accuracy, particularly given the assumption that more historical data always yields better predictions.

Method: The study employed multiple encoder types on clinical notes (BOW, count BOW, TF-IDF, LDA, BERT, 1D CNN, BiLSTM, Average) and evaluated prediction models using: (1) clinical notes alone, (2) structured data alone, and (3) combined modalities, across observation windows ranging from day of surgery to 3 years prior.

Result: Unstructured clinical notes achieved maximum predictive performance with a significantly shorter time window (3-6 months prior to surgery), while structured data performance improved with longer windows but plateaued after 12 months. These temporal patterns remained consistent regardless of model complexity or encoder type.

Conclusion: This study establishes targeted time-window guidelines for readmission prediction: unstructured clinical notes perform optimally with 3-6 month windows, while structured data benefits from extended windows but plateaus after 12 months. These modality-specific temporal patterns are robust across different model architectures.

Abstract: With the proliferation of Electronic Health Records (EHRs), a critical challenge in building predictive models is determining the optimal historical data time window to maximize accuracy. This study investigates the impact of various observation windows ranging from the day of surgery to three years prior on predicting 30-day readmission following hip and knee arthroplasties. The dataset encompasses both structured encounter records (over 4 million) and unstructured clinical notes (80,000) from 7,174 patients. To extract meaning from the clinical notes, we employed a suite of non neural (BOW, count BOW, TF IDF, LDA) and neural encoders (BERT, 1D CNN, BiLSTM, Average). We subsequently evaluated models utilizing clinical notes alone, structured data alone, and a combination of both modalities. Our results demonstrate that the optimal time window for unstructured clinical notes is significantly shorter than for structured data, maximum predictive performance was achieved using notes from just three to six months prior to surgery. In contrast, performance using structured data improved as the time window lengthened, but strictly plateaued after twelve months. These modality-specific temporal patterns remained consistent regardless of model complexity or encoder type. Ultimately, these findings challenge the general assumption that more historical data inherently yields better machine learning predictions, establishing targeted time-window guidelines for optimizing readmission prediction models.

</details>


### [120] [Learning the Helmholtz equation operator with DeepONet for non-parametric 2D geometries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00760)
*Rodolphe Barlogis,Ferhat Tamssaouet,Quentin Falcoz,Stéphane Grieu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: A physics-informed DeepONet framework for solving 2D Helmholtz equations with arbitrary scatterer geometries, using signed distance functions to encode domain geometry and local information for the scattered field operator.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing methods for solving Helmholtz equations with arbitrary scatterer geometries rely on FEM, which requires remeshing for each geometry and is computationally expensive. There is a need for efficient surrogate models that can handle non-parametric domains.

Method: Physics-informed DeepONet architecture using signed distance functions for geometry encoding (branch) and local information (trunk); trained to learn the operator mapping scatterer geometry to scattered field; evaluated against FEM solutions.

Result: The network generalizes well to unseen geometries when training space covers the target evaluation space. The physics is implicitly embedded in trained weights, and the model can be refined for new regions without retraining from scratch.

Conclusion: The proposed DeepONet-based approach provides a computationally efficient surrogate model that generalizes well to unseen geometries without requiring FEM-generated training data or domain remeshing. The framework supports refinement for new regions without full retraining.

Abstract: This paper deals with solving the 2D Helmholtz equation on non-parametric domains, leveraging a physics-informed neural operator network based on the DeepONet framework. We consider a 2D square domain with an inclusion of arbitrary boundary geometry at its center. This inclusion acts as a scatterer for an incoming harmonic wave. The aim is to learn the operator linking the geometry of the scatterer to the resulting scattered field. A signed distance function to the boundary of the inner inclusion, evaluated at several points in the domain, is used to encode its geometry. It serves as input for the branch part of the DeepONet architecture, while local information is used as input for the trunk part. This approach enables the encoding of arbitrary geometries, whether they are parameterized or not. The evaluation of the model on unseen geometries is compared with its finite element method (FEM) equivalent to test its generalization capabilities. The trained network weights implicitly embed the local physics and their interaction with the domain geometry. If the training space sufficiently covers the target evaluation space, the model can generalize accordingly. Furthermore, it can be refined to extend to another region of interest without retraining from scratch. This framework also avoids the need to remesh the domain for each geometry. The proposed approach delivers a computationally lighter surrogate model than FEM alternatives and avoids relying on FEM-generated training data.

</details>


### [121] [SAVGO: Learning State-Action Value Geometry with Cosine Similarity for Continuous Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00787)
*Stavros Orfanoudakis,Pedro P. Vergara*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SAVGO通过学习价值导向的几何空间，将表示学习、价值估计与策略优化统一于单一目标，利用相似性核引导策略向高价值区域优化，在MuJoCo高维任务上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前强化学习中，表示学习和相似性学习虽然提高了样本效率，但很少被直接用于塑造动作空间中的策略更新，存在理论与实践的脱节。

Method: SAVGO learns a joint state-action embedding space where action-value相似的动作对具有高余弦相似性，而不同的动作对被映射到不同方向。这一几何结构用于生成候选动作上的相似性核，使策略改进能够直接引导至比局部梯度更新更远的高价值区域。方法在单一几何一致目标下统一了表示学习、价值估计和策略优化，同时保持了离策略演员-评论家训练的可扩展性。

Result: 在标准MuJoCo连续控制基准测试中，SAVGO在具有挑战性的高维任务上相比强基线方法表现更优。

Conclusion: Ablation studies demonstrate the individual contributions of value-geometry learning and similarity-based policy updates, validating the proposed approach.

Abstract: While representation and similarity learning have improved the sample efficiency of Reinforcement Learning (RL), they are rarely used to shape policy updates directly in the action space. To bridge this gap, a geometry-aware RL algorithm that explicitly incorporates value-based similarity into the policy update, State-Action Value Geometry Optimization (SAVGO), is proposed. In detail, SAVGO learns a joint state-action embedding space in which pairs with similar action-value estimates exhibit high cosine similarity, while dissimilar pairs are mapped to distinct directions. This learned geometry enables the generation of a similarity kernel over candidate actions sampled at each update, allowing policy improvement to be guided directly toward higher-value regions beyond local gradient-based updates. As a result, representation learning, value estimation, and policy optimization are unified within a single geometry-consistent objective, while preserving the scalability of off-policy actor-critic training. The proposed method is evaluated on standard MuJoCo continuous-control benchmarks, demonstrating improvements over strong baselines on challenging high-dimensional tasks. Ablation studies are done to analyze the contributions of value-geometry learning and similarity-based policy updates.

</details>


### [122] [Generating Statistical Charts with Validation-Driven LLM Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00800)
*Pavlin G. Poličar,Andraž Pevcin,Blaž Zupan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一个结构化的LLM工作流，用于从表格数据生成多样化、可读性强的统计图表，并通过渲染验证解决可视化特有的失败模式，最终生成1,500张图表及30,003个问答对，用于评估16个多模态LLM的图表推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前LLM生成图表存在两大挑战：(1) 许多失败在渲染后才显现，仅从数据或代码无法检测；(2) 现有图表数据集缺乏完全对齐的产物（可执行代码、数据集上下文、问答对）。

Method: 工作流包含7个阶段：数据集筛选→图表提案→代码合成→渲染→验证驱动的改进→描述生成→问答生成。通过将图表生成视为可检查的过程而非一次性任务，保留每个图表的代码、数据集上下文、描述和问答对。

Result: 在UCI数据集上生成1,500张图表（来自74个数据集，24种图表类型），配对30,003个问答对。评估16个多模态LLM后发现：图表语法类问题近乎饱和，而数值提取、比较和推理类问题仍具挑战性。

Conclusion: 该工作流有效支持了图表多模态推理的诊断研究，揭示了当前MLLM在图表深层理解（而非表面语法）方面的能力边界。

Abstract: Generating diverse, readable statistical charts from tabular data remains challenging for LLMs, as many failures become apparent after rendering and are not detectable from data or code alone. Existing chart datasets also rarely provide fully aligned artifacts, such as executable code, dataset context, and question-answer pairs. We present a structured LLM-based workflow that decomposes chart generation into dataset screening, plot proposal, code synthesis, rendering, validation-driven refinement, description generation, and question-answer generation. By incorporating rendered-output validation, the workflow addresses visualization-specific failure modes such as readability and semantic mismatch. It treats chart generation as an inspectable process rather than a one-shot prompt-to-code task, retaining each chart with its code, dataset context, description, and question-answer pairs. Applied to UCI datasets, the workflow produces 1,500 charts from 74 datasets, spanning 24 chart families and paired with 30,003 question-answer pairs. We evaluate 16 multimodal LLMs (MLLMs) on these chart-question pairs. The results show that chart-syntax questions are nearly saturated, while value extraction, comparison, and reasoning remain more challenging, illustrating the workflow's utility for diagnostic studies of chart-grounded multimodal reasoning.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Cross-level Privacy Preserving Utility Mining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00036)
*Jiahong Cai,Wensheng Gan,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文研究跨层级隐私保护效用挖掘(CLPPUM)问题，针对泛化项目提出三种保护算法，并设计GI-dic字典结构提升效率。实验验证了方法的有效性，Min-RF算法整体性能最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实数据集常包含层级分类信息，现有隐私保护效用挖掘方法难以有效处理泛化项目，亟需针对跨层级场景的保护方案。

Method: 提出跨层级隐私保护效用挖掘(CLPPUM)问题，开发三种受害者项目选择算法(Min-RF、Max-RF、Best-NSCF)，并设计GI-dic字典结构以加速效用指标计算。

Result: 成功隐藏所有敏感跨层级高效用项集，未引入人工项集；在稀疏数据集上表现良好；Min-RF和Best-NSCF一致优于Max-RF；Min-RF在低效用阈值和稠密数据集上性能最佳。

Conclusion: Min-RF算法整体表现最佳，尤其在低效用阈值和稠密数据集中；GI-dic字典结构有效加速了度量计算；研究为层级分类数据场景下的隐私保护提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Privacy-preserving utility mining (PPUM) aims to hide sensitive high-utility patterns while preserving the utility of the sanitized database. In practice, however, many datasets are associated with taxonomic information, which makes the identification and processing of generalized items more challenging. To address this, we investigate the cross-level privacy-preserving utility mining (CLPPUM) problem and propose a method for protecting generalized items. Based on different victim item selection strategies, we develop three CLPPUM algorithms: minimum RGISU first (Min-RF), maximum RGISU first (Max-RF), and best NSC first (Best-NSCF). Furthermore, to enable efficient victim item identification, a novel dictionary structure named GI-dic is designed to accelerate the computation of required utility metrics. Experimental results on multiple datasets demonstrate that the proposed algorithms successfully hide all sensitive cross-level high-utility itemsets without introducing artificial itemsets. The results also show that our method performs well on sparse datasets, and both Min-RF and Best-NSCF consistently outperform Max-RF. Overall, Min-RF achieves the best performance, particularly when the minimum utility threshold is low and the dataset is dense.

</details>


### [124] [SiriusHelper: An LLM Agent-Based Operations Assistant for Big Data Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00043)
*Yu Shen,Shiyang Liu,Qihang He,Yihang Cheng,Haining Xie,Zhiming He,Huahua Fan,Xianzhi Tan,Teng Ma,Shaoquan Zhang,Danqing Huang,Fan Jiang,Yang Li,Chongqing Zhao,Peng Chen,Jie Jiang,Bin Cui*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: SiriusHelper是腾讯大数据平台部署的智能助手系统，通过意图识别路由、DeepSearch驱动多跳检索、分层知识库和自动化SOP提炼等机制，解决了大模型助手在企业实际部署中的场景覆盖、知识访问效率和维护成本问题，已在线上验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代企业中大数据平台广泛部署，但现有LLM+RAG助手面临三大实际挑战：1) 场景覆盖不足，难以同时覆盖通用咨询和领域特定故障排查工作流；2) 知识访问效率低，现有方法缺乏多跳检索能力且知识组织扁平；3) 维护成本高，升级工单无结构化难以转化为助手改进和可复用SOP。

Method: 1) 统一在线助手架构，自动识别用户意图并路由查询至适当处理路径，包括专项专家工作流；2) 结合DeepSearch驱动机制与基于优先级的分层知识库，实现多跳检索同时避免上下文过载；3) 引入自动化工单理解与SOP提炼机制，诊断助手失败原因并提取领域特定SOP以持续丰富知识库。

Result: SiriusHelper相比代表性替代方案表现更优，线上工单量降低20.8%，实现了更高的答案可靠性和更低的响应延迟。

Conclusion: SiriusHelper已成功部署于腾讯大数据平台，在性能上优于代表性替代方案，并将线上工单量减少20.8%，验证了其在实际生产环境中的有效性和实用价值。

Abstract: Big data platforms are widely used in modern enterprises, and an in-production intelligent assistant is increasingly important to help users quickly find actionable guidance and reduce operational burden. While recent LLM+RAG assistants provide a natural interface, they face practical challenges in real deployments: limited scenario coverage across both general consultation and domain-specific troubleshooting workflows, inefficient knowledge access due to inadequate multi-hop retrieval and flat knowledge organization, and high maintenance cost because escalated tickets are unstructured and hard to convert into assistant improvements and reusable SOPs.
  In this paper, we present SiriusHelper, a deployed intelligent assistant for big data platforms. SiriusHelper serves as a unified online assistant that automatically identifies user intent and routes queries to the right handling path, including dedicated expert workflows for specialized scenarios (e.g., SQL execution diagnosis). To support complex troubleshooting, SiriusHelper combines a DeepSearch-driven mechanism with a priority-based hierarchical knowledge base to enable multi-hop retrieval without context overload, thus improving answer reliability and latency. To reduce expert overhead, SiriusHelper further introduces automated ticket understanding and SOP distillation: it diagnoses the assistant failure reason (e.g., missing knowledge or wrong routing) and extracts domain-specific SOPs to continuously enrich the knowledge base. Experiments and online deployment on Tencent Big Data platform show that SiriusHelper outperforms representative alternatives and reduces online ticket volume by 20.8\%.

</details>


### [125] [Multiset semantics in SPARQL, Relational Algebra and Datalog](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00417)
*Renzo Angles,Claudio Gutierrez,Daniel Hernández*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: The paper establishes formal connections between SPARQL (with multiset semantics) and two well-known query languages—non-recursive Datalog with safe negation and multiset relational algebra—demonstrating that these three formalisms are expressively equivalent.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To analyze and characterize the algebraic and logical structure of SPARQL patterns involving AND, UNION, FILTER, EXCEPT, and SELECT under multiset semantics.

Method: Alignment with established query languages: (i) non-recursive Datalog with safe negation extended for multisets, and (ii) multiset relational algebra with projection, selection, natural join, arithmetic union, and except.

Result: Proved expressive equivalence between SPARQL, non-recursive Datalog, and multiset relational algebra.

Conclusion: The three formalisms (SPARQL, non-recursive Datalog with safe negation, and multiset relational algebra) are proved to be expressively equivalent under multiset semantics.

Abstract: The paper analyzes and characterizes the algebraic and logical structure of the multiset semantics for SPARQL patterns involving AND, UNION, FILTER, EXCEPT, and SELECT. To do this, we align SPARQL with two well-established query languages: Datalog and Relational Algebra. Specifically, we study (i) a version of non-recursive Datalog with safe negation extended to support multisets, and (ii) a multiset relational algebra comprising projection, selection, natural join, arithmetic union, and except. We prove that these three formalisms are expressively equivalent under multiset semantics.

</details>


### [126] [EGREFINE: An Execution-Grounded Optimization Framework for Text-to-SQL Schema Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00628)
*Jiaqian Wang,Yutao Qi,Wenjin Hou,Yu Pang,Rui Yang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: EGRefine addresses Text-to-SQL accuracy degradation caused by poor schema naming by reframing schema refinement as a constrained optimization problem. It uses a four-phase pipeline with execution-grounded verification and non-destructive SQL views, providing safety guarantees at column level while empirically handling cross-column interactions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Real-world database schemas suffer from ambiguous, abbreviated, or inconsistent naming conventions that degrade Text-to-SQL model accuracy. Existing approaches treat schemas as fixed and address errors downstream, lacking principled schema-level optimization.

Method: EGRefine: a four-phase pipeline combining column screening, context-aware candidate generation, execution-grounded verification, and view-based materialization. Uses column-wise greedy decomposition to handle computational hardness, with conservative selection rules for non-degradation guarantees.

Result: Across controlled and real-world benchmarks, EGRefine recovers accuracy lost to schema naming noise and correctly abstains when tasks exceed current capabilities. Refined schemas transfer across model families, enabling broad deployment benefits.

Conclusion: EGRefine provides a safe, generalizable schema refinement solution for Text-to-SQL systems. The view-based materialization ensures query equivalence while column-local guarantees prevent degradation. The approach enables "refine-once, serve-many-models" deployment across different model families.

Abstract: Text-to-SQL enables non-expert users to query databases in natural language, yet real-world schemas often suffer from ambiguous, abbreviated, or inconsistent naming conventions that degrade model accuracy. Existing approaches treat schemas as fixed and address errors downstream. In this paper, we frame schema refinement as a constrained optimization problem: find a renaming function that maximizes downstream Text-to-SQL execution accuracy while preserving query equivalence through database views. We analyze the computational hardness of this problem, which motivates a column-wise greedy decomposition, and instantiate it as EGRefine: a four-phase pipeline that screens ambiguous columns, generates context-aware candidate names, verifies them through execution-grounded feedback, and materializes the result as non-destructive SQL views. The pipeline carries two structural properties: column-local non-degradation, ensured by the conservative selection rule in the verification phase, and database-level query equivalence, ensured by the view-based materialization phase. Together they make the resulting refinement safe by construction at the column level, with cross-column and prompt-level interactions handled empirically rather than analytically. Across controlled schema-degradation, real-world, and enterprise benchmarks, EGRefine recovers accuracy lost to schema naming noise where applicable and correctly abstains where the underlying task exceeds current Text-to-SQL capabilities, with refined schemas transferring across model families to enable refine-once, serve-many-models deployment. Code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/ai-jiaqian/EGRefine.

</details>


### [127] [Living Databases: A Unified Model for Continuous Schema Evolution, Versioning, and Transformations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00676)
*Amol Deshpande*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 本文针对数据库和数据集中更新、转换、版本控制、模式变更等多样化演进机制长期被独立研究的现状，提出统一抽象框架，整合血统追踪、条件更新传播、可配置告警及从属对象演化控制，基于Prolly Tree实现原型系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前数据库和数据集管理领域中的多种功能（如更新、转换、版本控制、模式变更、流处理等）被独立研究，各自有其抽象、算法和系统实现，导致研究碎片化，缺乏统一视角。

Method: 提出了一个统一抽象框架，整合了系统级操作的血统追踪、条件更新传播、可配置的变化事件告警机制，以及声明式控制从属对象（如视图、ML模型）演化的机制。实现上采用Merkle树启发的"Prolly Tree"数据结构，支持可调参数以满足不同性能需求。

Result: 该框架具有足够强的表达能力，能够涵盖现有用例并支持新应用场景，同时实现了血统追踪、条件更新传播、可配置告警及从属对象演化控制的无缝集成。

Conclusion: 通过基于"Prolly Tree"的原型实现验证了框架可行性，并展示了初步实验结果，证明了所提方法的有效性。

Abstract: Databases, and datasets more generally, evolve continuously through updates, transformations, versioning, schema changes, streaming operations, and other mechanisms. While prior work has noted connections among some of these areas, they have traditionally been studied in isolation, each with its own abstractions, algorithms, and system implementations. In this paper, we argue for unifying these diverse functionalities under a single abstraction and a common set of computational primitives. We present such an abstraction, powerful enough to encompass existing use cases and to support new ones. Going beyond previous approaches, our framework seamlessly integrates provenance tracking for system-visible operations, conditional propagation of updates, and configurable alerts on change events. It also offers a principled treatment of dependent objects such as views and derived artifacts like machine learning models, by providing declarative mechanisms to control their evolution. Finally, we sketch a prototype implementation in a relational-like database system based on an adaptation of the "Prolly Tree", a Merkle tree-inspired data structure with tunable parameters to meet varying performance requirements, and present some initial experimental results.

</details>


### [128] [Complete Integration of Team Project-based Learning into a Database Syllabus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00736)
*S. Iserte,V. R. Tomas,M. Pérez,M. Castillo,P. Boronat,L. A. García*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: This study examines Team Project-Based Learning (TPBL) implementation in advanced database courses at Jaume I University over four years. TPBL combines project-based learning with teamwork to simulate real work conditions. The research documents project development processes and evaluates effectiveness through peer assessment, student feedback, and final grades.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional classroom learning often fails to adequately prepare students for real work environments. There is a need to combine project-based learning with teamwork to better simulate professional settings and enhance learning outcomes through collaborative problem-solving experiences.

Method: The study implemented TPBL in two advanced database subjects over four academic years (2018/19-2021/2022), documenting project development processes and evaluating results through three perspectives: peer teamwork evaluation, student feedback surveys, and final subject grades.

Result: Positive outcomes were achieved over the four-year implementation period, with the multi-perspective evaluation approach (peer assessment, student opinions, and grades) providing comprehensive validation of the TPBL effectiveness.

Conclusion: The positive outcomes from four years of implementation suggest TPBL is effective for advanced database courses, with multi-dimensional evaluation confirming its value in developing both soft and specific competencies.

Abstract: Team project-based learning (TPBL) combines two learning techniques: project-based learning (PBL) and teamwork. This combination leverages the learning outcomes of both methods and places students in a real work situation where they must develop and solve a real project while working as a team. TPBL has been used in two advanced database subjects in Jaume I University (UJI)'s Computer Science degree program. This learning method was used for four years (academic years from 2018/19 to 2021/2022) with positive outcomes. This study presents the project development, which includes teamwork formation, activities, timetable, and exercised learning competencies (both soft and specific). Further, the project's results were evaluated from three different perspectives: a) teamwork evaluation by teammates, b) Students' opinions on the subject and project, and c) subject final grades.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [129] [Exploring LLM biases to manipulate AI search overview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00012)
*Roman Smirnov*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: This paper demonstrates that LLM Overview systems are susceptible to manipulation through reinforcement learning-based snippet rewriting, revealing that these systems rely on comparative source advantages rather than absolute quality assessments.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs used in web search and LLM Overview systems have known biases. The source selection stage in these systems can be manipulated, which has implications for information integrity and safety.

Method: Train a small language model using reinforcement learning (RL) to rewrite search snippets, designed to increase their likelihood of being preferred by an LLM Overview system. The RL policy is constrained to operate only on snippets with limited reward-hacking strategies.

Result: RL-based snippet rewriting successfully manipulates LLM Overview selections in most cases. LLM Overview selections are driven by comparative (relative) advantages among candidate sources rather than absolute source quality.

Conclusion: Small language models can successfully manipulate LLM Overview selections through snippet rewriting, and context poisoning poses real safety threats. Defenses should focus on balancing comparative advantages among sources.

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) are used in many business applications in general, and specifically in web search systems and applications that generate overviews of search results - LLM Overview systems. Such systems are using an LLM to select most relevant sources from search results and generate an answer to the user's query. It is known from many studies that LLMs have different biases, in LLM Overview application both the source selection and answer generation stages may be affected by the biases of LLMs (here we are focusing mainly on the selection stage). This research is focused on investigating the presence of the biases in LLM Overview systems and on biases exploitation to manipulate LLM Overview results. Here we train a small language model using reinforcement learning to rewrite search snippets to increase their likelihood of being preferred by an LLM Overview. Our experimental setup intentionally restricts the policy to operate only on snippets and limits reward-hacking strategies, reflecting realistic constraints of web search environments. The results prove that LLM Overview systems have biases and that reinforcement learning in most of the cases can optimize snippet's content to manipulate LLM Overview results. We also prove that LLM Overview selections are driven by comparative rather than absolute advantages among candidate sources. In addition, we examine safety aspects of LLM Overview manipulation possibilities and show that context poisoning attacks can lead to inaccurate or harmful results.

</details>


### [130] [A Survey of Reasoning-Intensive Retrieval: Progress and Challenges](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00063)
*Yiyang Wei,Tingyu Song,Siyue Zhang,Yilun Zhao*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Reasoning-Intensive Retrieval (RIR) targets retrieval settings where relevance is mediated by latent inferential links between a query and supporting evidence, rather than semantic similarity. Motivated by the emergent reasoning abilities of Large Language Models (LLMs), recent work integrates these capabilities into the IR field, spanning the entire pipeline from benchmarks to retrievers and rerankers. Despite this progress, the field lacks a systematic framework to organize current efforts and articulate a clear path forward. To provide a clear roadmap for this rapidly growing yet fragmented area, this survey (1) systematizes existing RIR benchmarks by knowledge domains and modalities, providing a detailed analysis of the current landscape; (2) introduces a structured taxonomy that categorizes methods based on where and how reasoning is integrated into the retrieval pipeline, alongside an analysis of their trade-offs and practical applications; and (3) summarizes challenges and future directions to guide research in this evolving field.

</details>


### [131] [Intelligent Elastic Feature Fading: Enabling Model Retrain-Free Feature Efficiency Rollouts at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00324)
*Jieming Di,Xiaoyu Chen,Ying She,Siyu Wang,Lizzie Liu,Fenggang Wu,Jiaoying Mu,Tony Tsui,Amr Elroumy,Hsing Tang,Zewei Jiang,Qiao Yang,Lin Qi,Haibo Lin,Weifeng Cui,Daniel Li,Kapil Gupta,Shivendra Pratap Singh,Jie Zheng,Arnold Overwijk,Ling Leng,Sri Reddy,Robert Malkin,Rocky Liu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出IEFF系统，通过在服务时弹性控制特征覆盖率和分布，实现无需重训练的快速特征效率优化。该方法将效率相关rollout速度提升5倍，消除GPU开销，并通过渐进式衰减相比突然移除特征可减少50%以上的在线性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模排序系统依赖数千个来自多时间跨度用户行为的特征，传统方法需要模型重训练，导致迭代周期长（3-6个月）、GPU资源消耗大、 rollout吞吐量受限，严重制约了特征效率优化的迭代速度。

Method: IEFF系统在服务时弹性控制特征覆盖率和分布，支持增量式特征覆盖率调整，同时模型通过周期性训练进行自适应。该系统还集成了严格的安全护栏、可逆性机制和全面的监控体系，以确保大规模场景下的稳定性。

Result: IEFF将效率相关rollout速度提升5倍，消除重训练带来的GPU开销，实现更快的容量回收。离线与在线实验表明，逐步特征衰减相比突然移除特征可防止50-55%的在线性能下降，同时维持稳定的模型行为。

Conclusion: 弹性特征衰减是一种实用且可扩展的方法，可用于管理现代工业排序系统中的特征效率。该方法通过在服务时控制特征覆盖率和分布，避免了传统的模型重训练周期，为大规模排序系统的特征管理提供了新的基础设施范式。

Abstract: Large-scale ranking systems depend on thousands of features derived from user behavior across multiple time horizons. Typically requires model retraining -- resulting in long iteration cycles (3--6 months), substantial GPU resource consumption, and limited rollout throughput.
  We introduce Intelligent Elastic Feature Fading (IEFF), a production infrastructure system that enables retrain-free feature efficiency rollouts by elastically controlling feature coverage and distribution at serving time. IEFF supports incremental feature coverage adjustments while models adapt through recurring training, eliminating dependencies on explicit retraining cycles. The system incorporates strict safety guardrails, reversibility mechanisms, and comprehensive monitoring to ensure stability at scale.
  Across multiple production use cases, IEFF accelerates efficiency-related rollouts by 5$\times$, eliminates retraining-related GPU overhead, and enables faster capacity recycling. Extensive offline and online experiments demonstrate that gradual feature fading prevents 50--55\% of online performance degradation compared to abrupt feature removal, while maintaining stable model behavior. These results establish elastic, system-level feature fading as a practical and scalable approach for managing feature efficiency in modern industrial ranking systems.

</details>


### [132] [DynamicPO: Dynamic Preference Optimization for Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00327)
*Xingyu Hu,Kai Zhang,Jiancan Wu,Shuli Wang,Chi Wang,Wenshuai Chen,Yinhua Zhu,Haitao Wang,Xingxing Wang,Xiang Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究发现了多负样本偏好优化中的"优化崩溃"现象，从理论和实证角度证明其源于易区分负样本对边界关键负样本的梯度抑制。提出了DynamicPO框架，通过自适应机制优先处理决策边界附近的负样本，并动态调整优化强度，从而有效防止性能退化，提升推荐精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 发现了一个反直觉的现象：在LLM推荐系统中，使用多负样本目标函数进行直接偏好优化（DPO）时，增加负样本数量反而导致性能下降，尽管训练损失持续降低。这种"偏好优化崩溃"现象尚未被充分研究和解决。

Method: 提出了DynamicPO框架，包含两个核心机制：1）动态边界负样本选择：识别并优先处理靠近模型决策边界的具有信息量的负样本；2）双边界动态beta调整：根据边界模糊度为每个样本动态校准优化强度。

Result: 在三个公开数据集上的实验表明，DynamicPO有效防止了优化崩溃，并提升了多负样本偏好优化方法的推荐精度。

Conclusion: 通过动态边界负样本选择和双边界动态beta调整两大自适应机制，DynamicPO有效解决了多负样本场景下的优化崩溃问题，显著提升推荐精度，且计算开销可忽略。

Abstract: In large language model (LLM)-based recommendation systems, direct preference optimization (DPO) effectively aligns recommendations with user preferences, requiring multi-negative objective functions to leverage abundant implicit-feedback negatives and sharpen preference boundaries. However, our empirical analyses reveal a counterintuitive phenomenon, preference optimization collapse, where increasing the number of negative samples can lead to performance degradation despite a continuously decreasing training loss. We further theoretically demonstrate that this collapse arises from gradient suppression, caused by the dominance of easily discriminable negatives over boundary-critical negatives that truly define user preference boundaries. As a result, boundary-relevant signals are under-optimized, weakening the model's decision boundary. Motivated by these observations, we propose DynamicPO (Dynamic Preference Optimization), a lightweight and plug-and-play framework comprising two adaptive mechanisms: Dynamic Boundary Negative Selection, which identifies and prioritizes informative negatives near the model's decision boundary, and Dual-Margin Dynamic beta Adjustment, which calibrates optimization strength per sample according to boundary ambiguity. Extensive experiments on three public datasets show that DynamicPO effectively prevents optimization collapse and improves recommendation accuracy on multi-negative preference optimization methods, with negligible computational overhead. Our code and datasets are available at https://github.com/xingyuHuxingyu/DynamicPO.

</details>


### [133] [Negative Data Mining for Contrastive Learning in Dense Retrieval at IKEA.com](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00353)
*Eva Agapaki,Amritpal Singh Gill*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对IKEA产品搜索场景，提出结合产品层级分类与属性的结构化负采样策略，以及基于LLM的全量候选产品相关性评估方法。离线实验准确率提升2.6%，但在线A/B测试无显著差异，提示需将用户搜索行为（尤其是零点击模式）纳入训练数据构建与评估体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对比学习的有效性高度依赖训练时负样本的质量。现有方法在负采样上存在不足：（1）缺乏针对产品层级结构和属性的结构化负采样策略；（2）缺乏可扩展的全量产品相关性标注方法。

Method: （1）基于产品层级分类体系和属性信息的结构化负采样策略，生成语义上具有挑战性的负样本；（2）基于LLM的评估方法，对所有候选产品进行相关性打分，替代稀疏人工标注或随机采样。

Result: 离线实验中平均类别准确率提升+2.6%；但在线A/B测试在长尾查询上用户参与度指标无统计显著差异。发现67%热门搜索零点击率超50%。

Conclusion: 离线实验中模型在平均类别准确率上提升+2.6%，但A/B测试在长尾查询上用户参与度指标无显著差异（p > 0.05）。研究发现67%的热门搜索零点击率超过50%，表明训练数据与离线评估需更贴近真实用户搜索行为，包括查询意图分布和零点击模式。

Abstract: Contrastive learning is a core component of modern retrieval systems, but its effectiveness heavily relies on the quality of negative examples used during training. In this work, we present a systematic approach to improving dense retrieval for IKEA product search through structured negative sampling strategies and scalable LLM-as-a-judge relevance evaluation.
  Building on IKEA Search Engine's late-interaction retrieval architectures, we introduce two key contributions: (1) structured negative sampling strategies that leverage product hierarchical taxonomy and product attributes to generate semantically challenging negatives, and (2) a comprehensive LLM-based evaluation methodology for generating training data. Rather than relying on sparse human annotations or random sampling, our LLM-based evaluation system allocates a score for all candidate products against each query.
  Our methodology achieves +2.6\% average category accuracy on offline real user query experiments on the Canada market. However, our A/B test on long-tail queries showed no statistically significant differences in user engagement metrics between the improved and baseline models ($p > 0.05$). We trace this gap to user search behavior: 67\% of popular searches exhibit zero-click rates above 50\%, indicating that a substantial proportion of search sessions result in no product engagement regardless of result ranking.
