These findings underscore the importance of hard negative mining but also the need for grounding training data and offline evals in real user search behavior -- including query intent distribution and zero-click patterns -- to bridge the gap between offline retrieval quality and online user engagement.

</details>


### [134] [FollowTable: A Benchmark for Instruction-Following Table Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00400)
*Rihui Jin,Yuchen Lu,Ting Zhang,Jun Wang,Kuicai Dong,Zhaocheng Du,Dongping Liu,Gang Wang,Yong Liu,Guilin Qi*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了指令跟随表格检索（IFTR）新任务，通过FollowTable基准测试和指令响应评分进行系统评估，揭示现有检索模型在处理表格数据的细粒度指令时存在明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Traditional Table Retrieval (TR) relies on topical semantic similarity, but LLM-based agentic systems require instruction-driven access to structured data where relevance is conditional on explicit content and schema constraints rather than topical similarity alone.

Method: (i) Proposed FollowTable benchmark built via taxonomy-driven annotation pipeline; (ii) introduced Instruction Responsiveness Score metric; (iii) conducted systematic evaluation of existing retrieval models.

Result: Existing retrieval models struggle to follow fine-grained instructions over tabular data, exhibiting systematic biases toward surface-level semantic cues and limited schema-grounded constraint handling.

Conclusion: Existing retrieval models struggle with IFTR, exhibiting systematic biases toward surface-level semantic cues and remaining limited in handling schema-grounded constraints, highlighting substantial room for future improvements.

Abstract: Table Retrieval (TR) has traditionally been formulated as an ad-hoc retrieval problem, where relevance is primarily determined by topical semantic similarity. With the growing adoption of LLM-based agentic systems, access to structured data is increasingly instruction-driven, where relevance is conditional on explicit content and schema constraints rather than topical similarity alone. We therefore formalize Instruction-Following Table Retrieval (IFTR), a new task that requires models to jointly satisfy topical relevance and fine-grained instruction constraints. We identify two core challenges in IFTR: (i) sensitivity to content scope, such as inclusion and exclusion constraints, and (ii) awareness of schema-grounded requirements, including column semantics and representation granularity--capabilities largely absent in existing retrievers. To support systematic evaluation, we introduce FollowTable, the first large-scale benchmark for IFTR, constructed via a taxonomy-driven annotation pipeline. We further propose a new metric, termed the Instruction Responsiveness Score, to evaluate whether retrieval rankings consistently adapt to user instructions relative to a topic-only baseline. Our results indicate that existing retrieval models struggle to follow fine-grained instructions over tabular data. In particular, they exhibit systematic biases toward surface-level semantic cues and remain limited in handling schema-grounded constraints, highlighting substantial room for future improvements.

</details>


### [135] [Time-Interval-Aware Disentangled Expert Modeling for Next-Basket Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00499)
*Zhiying Deng,Yuan Fu,Usman Farooq,Ziwei Tian,Wei Liu,Jianjun Li*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TIDE框架，通过Hawkes增强的时间编码和双专家架构解决NBR中的意图纠缠和时间建模问题，实现复购与探索的有效平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NBR方法存在两个局限：(1) 将冲突的用户意图（习惯性复购与探索性兴趣）纠缠在单一表示中，导致习惯覆盖发现；(2) 依赖离散序列建模，忽视连续时间间隔和项目特定的时间周期性。

Method: 提出Time-Interval Disentangled Experts (TIDE)方法，包含三部分核心组件：(1) Hawkes增强的傅里叶时间编码，捕获项目特定的时间周期性和动态衰减；(2) 双专家架构，分别处理习惯性复购（Habit Expert）和模式引导的探索（Pattern-Guided Exploration Expert）；(3) 项目感知门控机制，自适应平衡复购与探索。

Result: 在四个不同的真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，TIDE一致性地优于代表性的先进NBR方法。

Conclusion: TIDE通过解耦用户意图并结合项目特定的时间周期建模，有效解决了现有NBR方法的局限性，在四个真实数据集上持续超越现有方法。

Abstract: Next-basket recommendation (NBR) is a type of recommendation that aims to predict a set of items a user will purchase based on their historical transaction basket sequences. It is governed by a dynamic interplay between two distinct user intents: habitual repurchase, which involves repeating past behaviors, and exploratory interest, which involves discovering new items. However, existing NBR methods generally suffer from two limitations: (1) they often entangle these conflicting motives within a single representation, causing habits to overshadow discovery, and (2) they rely on discrete sequential modeling that ignores continuous-time intervals and item-specific periodicities. In this paper, we propose a novel solution named Time-Interval Disentangled Experts (TIDE) to address these challenges. TIDE incorporates a Hawkes-enhanced Fourier Time Encoding to capture item-specific temporal periodicities and dynamic decay. To decouple user intentions, TIDE utilizes a dual-expert architecture that integrates a Habit Expert for recurring needs and a Pattern-Guided Exploration Expert for discovery. Combined with an item-aware gating mechanism, TIDE adaptively balances repurchase and exploration. Extensive experiments on four diverse real-world datasets demonstrate that TIDE consistently outperforms representative state-of-the-art NBR methods.

</details>


### [136] [LLM-Oriented Information Retrieval: A Denoising-First Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00505)
*Lu Dai,Liang Sun,Fanpu Cao,Ziyang Rao,Cehao Yang,Hao Liu,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文是一篇观点论文，探讨了在大语言模型（LLM）时代，信息检索范式从服务人类转向服务LLM的根本性转变。作者提出"去噪"正成为信息访问全流程的核心瓶颈，并构建了从"不可访问"到"不可验证"的四阶段挑战框架，同时提供了信号噪声优化的技术分类体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统信息检索主要服务于人类用户，但在RAG和agentic搜索场景下，LLM成为主要消费者。LLM受限于注意力预算，且对噪声（误导性或不相关信息）极其敏感——这些噪声不仅造成困扰，更直接导致幻觉和推理失败。因此需要重新审视信息检索的核心挑战。

Method: 本文采用概念框架构建和综述的方法：(1) 提出四阶段IR挑战框架：不可访问( inaccessible) → 不可发现(undiscoverable) → 错配(misaligned) → 不可验证(unverifiable)；(2) 按流程组织信号噪声优化技术分类体系，覆盖索引、检索、上下文工程、验证和agentic工作流；(3) 综述了终身助手、编码Agent、深度研究、多模态理解等依赖检索的领域中的去噪研究。

Result: 识别出信息访问管道中"可用证据密度"和"可验证性"是核心瓶颈；构建了涵盖五个层面的去噪技术分类（索引优化、检索优化、上下文工程、验证机制、agentic工作流）；明确了从传统IR到LLM-centric IR的范式转变。

Conclusion: 去噪（即在有限上下文窗口内最大化可用证据密度和可验证性）正成为信息访问全流程的主要瓶颈。本文框架为理解这一范式转变提供了系统性视角，并为未来RAG和检索增强系统的研究指明方向。

Abstract: Modern information retrieval (IR) is no longer consumed primarily by humans but increasingly by large language models (LLMs) via retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) and agentic search. Unlike human users, LLMs are constrained by limited attention budgets and are uniquely vulnerable to noise; misleading or irrelevant information is no longer just a nuisance, but a direct cause of hallucinations and reasoning failures. In this perspective paper, we argue that denoising-maximizing usable evidence density and verifiability within a context window-is becoming the primary bottleneck across the full information access pipeline. We conceptualize this paradigm shift through a four-stage framework of IR challenges: from inaccessible to undiscoverable, to misaligned, and finally to unverifiable. Furthermore, we provide a pipeline-organized taxonomy of signal-to-noise optimization techniques, spanning indexing, retrieval, context engineering, verification, and agentic workflow. We also present research works on information denoising in domains that rely heavily on retrieval such as lifelong assistant, coding agent, deep research, and multimodal understanding.

</details>


### [137] [MUDY: Multi-Granular Dynamic Candidate Contextualization for Unsupervised Keyphrase Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00597)
*Hyeongu Kang,Susik Yoon*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: MUDY proposes a context-centric keyphrase extraction framework that captures multi-granular contextual salience using dual scoring mechanisms—prompt-based scoring with candidate-aware weighting and self-attention-based scoring—demonstrating superior performance over state-of-the-art methods on multiple datasets.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing PLM-based keyphrase extraction methods excel at global semantic relevance but fail to capture local contextual importance tied to subtopics scattered throughout documents.

Method: The framework employs two complementary components: (1) prompt-based scoring with candidate-aware weighting for local contextual importance, and (2) self-attention-based scoring using multi-granular attention patterns to assess significance at both document-wide and segment-specific levels.

Result: Evaluations on four real-world datasets confirm MUDY's superiority in top-k accuracy; qualitative and quantitative analyses validate the effectiveness of multi-granular saliency modeling.

Conclusion: MUDY achieves superior performance over state-of-the-art baselines in top-k accuracy at various cutoff thresholds, with source code publicly available.

Abstract: Keyphrase extraction aims to automatically identify concise phrases that effectively represent the content of a document. While recent methods leveraging pre-trained language models (PLMs) have significantly improved the extraction of keyphrases with strong global semantic relevance, they often fall short in capturing the local contextual importance of keyphrases tied to specific subtopics dispersed in a document. In this paper, we propose a novel context-centric framework, MUDY, that effectively captures multi-granular contextual salience of candidate keyphrases. MUDY employs two complementary components: (1) a prompt-based scoring that estimates the generation likelihood of each candidate keyphrase, augmented with candidate-aware weighting to better reflect its local contextual importance, and (2) a self-attention-based scoring that utilizes multi-granular attention patterns from PLMs to assess candidate significance at both the document-wide and segment-specific levels. Evaluations on four real-world datasets demonstrate that MUDY outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in top-k accuracy at various cutoff thresholds. In-depth quantitative and qualitative analyses further highlight the efficacy of context-centric keyphrase extraction with multi-granular saliency. For reproducibility, the source code of MUDY is available at https://github.com/HgKang1/MUDY.

</details>


### [138] [A Replicability Study of XTR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00646)
*Rohan Jha,Reno Kriz,Benjamin Van Durme*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文复现并扩展评估了XTR算法，发现在受控比较下XTR相比ColBERT无整体有效性优势，但其训练修改通过展平token分数分布，为IVF-based检索引擎带来效率提升，扩展了XTR训练在多种部署场景中的实用价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始XTR算法通过修改ColBERT检索来避免高昂的完整嵌入聚合和重排步骤，通过插补初始token检索步骤中缺失的相似度分数来提升效率。原始工作认为标准ColBERT token评分不适用于插补，因此提出了修改后的训练目标。

Method: 复现XTR检索算法及其改进训练目标，并扩展评估至知识蒸馏(KD)训练和高效检索引擎(PLAID和WARP)。

Result: （1）确认了原始工作中声称的token级匹配特征；（2）在受控比较下未能复现XTR相比ColBERT的整体有效性优势；（3）发现XTR训练修改对现代检索引擎有具体的机制性影响：通过展平ColBERT特征性的尖峰token分数分布，XTR训练产生更具判别性的质心分数，从而在PLAID和WARP下实现更高效的IVF检索。

Conclusion: XTR训练方法的实用价值不限于原始研究的低k'场景，而是扩展到任何使用IVF-based检索引擎的部署环境。这些发现为从业者提供了关于何时以及如何使用XTR作为多向量检索器的具体指导。

Abstract: The XTR (conteXtual Token Retrieval) algorithm is a modification to ColBERT retrieval that avoids the costly step of fully gathering and reranking the candidates' embeddings by imputing their missing similarity scores from the initial token retrieval step. The original work proposes a modified training objective as necessary for effective XTR retrieval, arguing that standard ColBERT token scoring is unsuitable for imputation. In this paper, we replicate both the XTR retrieval algorithm and its modified training objective, and extend the evaluation to knowledge-distillation (KD) training and efficient retrieval engines (PLAID and WARP). We confirm the token-level matching characteristics claimed in the original work, but fail to replicate XTR's overall effectiveness advantage over ColBERT under a controlled comparison. We further show that XTR's training modification has a concrete mechanistic consequence for modern retrieval engines: by flattening ColBERT's characteristically peaked token score distribution, XTR training yields more discriminative centroid scores and thus more efficient IVF-based retrieval under PLAID and WARP. The utility of XTR training is therefore not limited to the low-$k'$ regime originally studied, but extends to any deployment setting where IVF-based engines are used. These findings offer practitioners concrete guidance on how and when to use XTR as their multi-vector retriever.

</details>


### [139] [Robust Multimodal Recommendation via Graph Retrieval-Enhanced Modality Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00670)
*Yuan Li,Jun Hu,Jiaxin Jiang,Bryan Hooi,Bingsheng He*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出GRE-MC框架，通过模态感知子图检索选取语义相关上下文，并结合图变换器与稀疏路由码本实现缺失模态的鲁棒重建，在多模态推荐任务中取得了显著的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实多模态推荐系统常面临模态不完整问题（传感器故障、标注稀缺、隐私限制等导致），现有模态补全方法仅从节点自身或其邻居推断信息，忽略了图中其他语义相关的上下文线索，这些线索难以通过简单的邻居聚合捕获。

Method: GRE-MC框架包含三个核心组件：(1) 模态感知子图检索机制，从全局图中选取与查询节点语义相关的子图，提供丰富的上下文信息；(2) 图变换器通过全局注意力机制联合编码查询节点与检索到的子图，完成缺失特征重建；(3) 可学习的稀疏路由码本将潜在嵌入正则化为紧凑基向量，增强模型鲁棒性。

Result: 在多个多模态推荐基准数据集上的实验表明，GRE-MC一致性地超越了现有最优方法，验证了子图检索与联合编码图变换器在鲁棒模态补全中的有效性。

Conclusion: GRE-MC通过子图检索与图变换器的协同设计，为多模态推荐系统中的缺失模态重建提供了一种可扩展且鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Multimodal data plays a critical role in web-based recommendation systems, where information from diverse modalities such as vision and text enhances representation learning. However, real-world multimodal datasets often suffer from modality incompleteness due to sensor failures, annotation scarcity, or privacy constraints, which substantially degrade model performance and reliability. One effective solution to address this issue is modality completion, which reconstructs missing features to provide modality-complete graphs for downstream tasks. Given a query node with missing multimodal features, existing modality completion methods typically infer information from the node itself or its neighbors to reconstruct the missing modality. However, these methods may overlook semantically relevant context in the graph, which contains valuable cues that are non-trivial to capture through simple methods like neighborhood aggregation. In this work, we propose GRE-MC, a Graph Retrieval-Enhanced Modality Completion framework, to overcome these limitations. By introducing a modality-aware subgraph retrieval mechanism, GRE-MC selects semantically relevant subgraphs from the entire graph, providing richer contextual information for completing missing modalities. Subsequently, a graph transformer jointly encodes the query node and the retrieved subgraph via global attention to complete the missing features, while a learnable sparse-routing codebook regularizes latent embeddings into compact bases for improved robustness. Extensive experiments on multimodal recommendation benchmarks demonstrate that GRE-MC consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, validating the effectiveness of subgraph retrieval and joint-encoding graph transformer for robust modality completion.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [The $\textit{Silicon Society}$ Cookbook: Design Space of LLM-based Social Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00197)
*Aurélien Bück-Kaeffer,Sneheel Sarangi,Maximilian Puelma Touzel,Reihaneh Rabbany,Zachary Yang,Jean-François Godbout*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: This paper systematically investigates design choices in LLM-based simulated social networks ("Silicon Societies"), finding that the design space has non-trivial geometry — parameters interact in both additive and complex ways — and that the choice of base LLM is the single most important determinant of simulation outcomes.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The design space of LLM-only social networks remains under-studied, creating a gap in validating model realism for Silicon Societies simulations.

Method: Systematic analysis of design choices in simulated social networks, varying base models and connection architectures, using surveys as proxy for agent opinions.

Result: Base LLM choice is the most critical factor; other parameters show both additive and complex non-additive interactions in shaping simulation outcomes.

Conclusion: The geometry of the design space is non-trivial, with additive and complex interactions among parameters. Base LLM choice is the most important variable impacting simulation outcomes.

Abstract: Studies attempting to simulate human behavior with $\textit{Silicon Societies}$ grow in numbers while LLM-only social networks have started appearing outside of controlled settings. However, the design space of these networks remains under-studied, which contributes to a gap in validating model realism. To enable future works to make more informed design decisions, we perform a systematic analysis of the consequences and interactions of key design choices in simulated social networks, including the choice of base model used to model individual agents, and how they are connected to each other. Using surveys as a proxy for agent opinions, our findings suggest that the geometry of the design space is non-trivial, with some parameters behaving in additive ways while others display more complex interactions. In particular, the choice of the base LLM is the most important variable impacting the simulation outcomes.

</details>


### [141] [Foresight Arena: An On-Chain Benchmark for Evaluating AI Forecasting Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00420)
*Maksym Nechepurenko,Pavel Shuvalov*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: Foresight Arena是首个链上、去中心化的AI预测能力评估平台，通过智能合约和Proper Scoring Rules（Brier Score + Alpha Score）在真实预测市场上评估AI智能体。提供了严格的理论分析（方差、Murphy分解、功效分析）和50轮实证验证，为AI预测能力评估提供了激励兼容、抗污染的技术框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI预测评估基准存在两大缺陷：静态数据集易受训练数据污染问题影响，而交易盈亏指标混淆了预测准确性与时机选择、仓位管理和风险偏好。需要构建抗过拟合、去中心化信任和激励兼容的评估环境。

Method: 基于Polygon PoS链上Solidity智能合约实现commit-reveal协议，利用Gnosis Conditional Token Framework进行去中心化结果结算，通过Brier Score和创新的Alpha Score（衡量相对于市场共识的预测优势）评估AI智能体的预测能力。进行了50轮、每轮7个市场的实时评估实验。

Result: 提供了Alpha Score的封闭形式方差分析，建立了与Murphy经典Brier分解的数学联系，并完成功效分析确定可靠区分不同技能水平所需的预测轮数。实证结果表明Murphy分解可有效区分校准良好的智能体与跟踪市场但缺乏分辨率能力的智能体。

Conclusion: Foresight Arena通过智能合约实现了去中心化的AI预测能力评估框架，结合Brier Score和Alpha Score两种评分规则，能够可靠区分不同技能水平的AI预测智能体。检测α*=0.02的边际优势需要约350个预测结果（约50轮×7个市场），而α*=0.01的边际优势则需要四倍的样本量。所有智能合约和评估基础设施已开源，为AI预测能力评估提供了可复现的技术基础。

Abstract: Evaluating the true forecasting ability of AI agents requires environments resistant to overfitting, free from centralized trust, and grounded in incentive-compatible scoring. Existing benchmarks either rely on static datasets vulnerable to training-data contamination, or measure trading PnL -- a metric conflating predictive accuracy with timing, sizing, and risk appetite. We introduce Foresight Arena, the first permissionless, on-chain benchmark for evaluating AI forecasting agents on real-world prediction markets. Agents submit probabilistic forecasts on binary Polymarket markets via a commit-reveal protocol enforced by Solidity smart contracts on Polygon PoS; outcomes are resolved trustlessly through the Gnosis Conditional Token Framework. Performance is measured by the Brier Score and a novel Alpha Score -- proper scoring rules that incentivize honest probability reporting and isolate predictive edge over market consensus. We provide a formal analysis: closed-form variance for per-market Alpha, the connection to Murphy's classical Brier decomposition, and a power analysis characterizing the number of rounds required to reliably distinguish agents of different skill levels. We show that detecting a true edge of $α^* = 0.02$ at 80% power requires approximately 350 resolved binary predictions (50 rounds of 7 markets), while $α^* = 0.01$ requires four times more. We complement these analytical results with a 50-round live evaluation of five frontier LLM agents plus a random baseline. Murphy decomposition distinguishes well-calibrated agents from market-tracking agents that fail through reduced resolution. All smart contracts and evaluation infrastructure are open-source.

</details>


### [142] [Learning to Act and Cooperate for Distributed Black-Box Consensus Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00691)
*Zi-Bo Qin,Feng-Feng Wei,Tai-You Chen,Wei-Neng Chen*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: This paper proposes LAC-MAS, a trajectory-driven LLM-based framework that enables multi-agent systems to self-design their internal action behaviors and external cooperation patterns for distributed blackbox consensus optimization, achieving superior performance across benchmarks compared to handcrafted baseline methods.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing distributed blackbox consensus optimization methods rely on handcrafted update rules and static cooperation patterns, which struggle to balance local adaptation, global coordination, and communication efficiency in heterogeneous nonconvex environments.

Method: LAC-MAS employs LLM-driven sparse high-level guidance to co-design agent-internal action behaviors and agent-external cooperation patterns from historical optimization trajectories, with a phased cognitive scheduling strategy enabling resource-aware activation of different adaptation forms.

Result: Experiments on standard distributed blackbox benchmarks and real-world distributed tasks show that LAC-MAS consistently improves solution quality, convergence efficiency, and communication efficiency over strong baselines.

Conclusion: LAC-MAS demonstrates a practical pathway from manually designed distributed coordination toward self-designing multi-agent optimization systems, effectively addressing the fundamental trade-off between local adaptation, global coordination, and communication efficiency.

Abstract: Distributed blackbox consensus optimization is a fundamental problem in multi-agent systems, where agents must improve a global objective using only local objective queries and limited neighbor communication. Existing methods largely rely on handcrafted update rules and static cooperation patterns, which often struggle to balance local adaptation, global coordination, and communication efficiency in heterogeneous nonconvex environments. In this paper, we take an initial step toward trajectory-driven self-design for distributed black-box consensus optimization. We first redesign the agent-level swarm dynamics with an adaptive internal mechanism tailored to decentralized consensus settings, improving the balance between exploration, convergence, and local escape. Built on top of this adaptive execution layer, we propose Learning to Act and Cooperate (LACMAS), a trajectorydriven framework in which large language models provide sparse highlevel guidance for shaping both agentinternal action behaviors and agentexternal cooperation patterns from historical optimization trajectories. We further introduce a phased cognitive scheduling strategy to activate different forms of adaptation in a resource-aware manner. Experiments on standard distributed black-box benchmarks and real-world distributed tasks show that LAC-MAS consistently improves solution quality, convergence efficiency, and communication efficiency over strong baselines, suggesting a practical route from handcrafted distributed coordination toward self-designing multi-agent optimization systems.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [143] [DEPTEX: Organization-First, Open Source Dependency Risk Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00179)
*Henry Ruckman-Utting,Vrushal Nedungadi,Taiga Okuma,LeTian Wang,Stephen Ehebald,Mohammad A. Tayebi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Deptex是一个组织优先的图计算平台，通过EPD技术（融合CPG切片与LLM语义验证）精确评估开源依赖漏洞的实际影响范围，并通过可编程治理引擎实现灵活的合规策略定制，解决了传统SCA工具告警过载和刚性合规的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SCA和可达性分析工具将风险视为组件固有属性，忽略语义上下文，导致告警过载；强制企业采用刚性合规框架，无法适应组织特定需求。

Method: 提出Execution Path Dominance (EPD)方法，融合Code Property Graph (CPG)切片与大语言模型(LLM)语义验证，量化漏洞的实际运行爆炸半径；设计可编程的"As Code"治理引擎，支持动态pull request策略、自定义资产分层和外部API集成。

Result: 实现从被动扫描向上下文感知治理的转变，支持主动式、高效且对齐的供应链风险管理。

Conclusion: Deptex通过将风险视为涌现性特征而非固有属性，实现了从被动扫描到主动治理的范式转变。EPD融合CPG切片与LLM语义验证，可精确计算漏洞的实际影响范围；As Code治理引擎使企业能自定义合规策略。这为大规模供应链风险管理提供了更智能、更灵活的解决方案。

Abstract: Open-source software (OSS) dependencies introduce systemic risks that are difficult to manage at scale. Existing Software Composition Analysis (SCA) and reachability tools generate severe alert fatigue by treating risk as an intrinsic component property, ignoring semantic context and forcing enterprises into rigid compliance frameworks. We present Deptex, an organization-first, graph-based platform treating supply chain risk as emergent. Deptex introduces Execution Path Dominance (EPD), fusing Code Property Graph (CPG) slicing with Large Language Model (LLM) semantic verification to calculate a vulnerability's true operational blast radius. To handle bespoke compliance, Deptex abstracts governance into a programmable ``As Code'' engine, enabling security teams to natively enforce dynamic pull request policies, custom asset tiers, and external API integrations. By shifting from reactive scanning to context-aware governance, Deptex enables proactive, efficient, and aligned supply chain risk management.

</details>


### [144] [What Characterizes a Software Leader? Identifying Leadership Practices from Practitioners Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00191)
*Murilo Coelho,Denivan Campos,Mariana Maia Bezerra,Matheus Paixao,Allysson Allex Araujo,Savio Freire*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对Dev.to社区116篇从业者文章的内容分析，识别出103项软件项目领导力实践，归类为五大维度。研究表明软件领导力主要关联管理和人际技能而非技术专长，为理解软件开发情境中的领导力提供了概念框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究主要关注正式角色和预定义领导力模型，对软件开发从业者在日常实践中如何体验和展示领导力缺乏深入了解。

Method: 采用内容分析法，对Dev.to在线社区上116篇从业者撰写的文章进行系统收集、筛选和编码，提取从业者叙述中的领导力实践，通过相关性分析进行分类和整合。

Result: 识别出103项软件项目领导者实践，区分推荐和反对的实践，并归类为五个维度：人员管理与开发、流程与执行、专业与个人成长、沟通与表达、战略愿景。最常被推荐的实践包括培养人际交往技能、管理与授权团队工作、练习管理技能；最常被反对的实践包括微观管理、效率低下的工作模式和沟通方式。所有实践被组织成概念地图。

Conclusion: 软件领导力主要与管理和人际交往实践相关，而非技术专长。研究构建的概念地图可作为理解软件开发情境中领导力的参考框架。

Abstract: Context: Leadership has been extensively studied in management and agile software development; however, prior research predominantly focuses on formal roles and predefined leadership models, offering limited insight into how leadership is experienced and demonstrated by software practitioners in everyday practice. Objective: Our goal is to identify and categorize leadership practices as perceived and reported by software development practitioners based on their professional experiences. Method: We conducted a content analysis of 116 practitioner-authored articles published on the Dev.to online community. Articles were systematically collected, screened, and coded, resulting in the extraction, correlation analysis and categorization of leadership practices grounded in practitioners narratives. Results: We identified 103 practices for software project leaders, distinguished between recommended and discouraged ones. These practices were organized into five categories: People Management & Development, Processes & Execution, Professional & Personal Growth, Communication & Articulation and Strategic Vision. The most recurrent recommended practices include Cultivating & Practicing Interpersonal Skills, Managing & Delegating Team Work, and Practicing & Developing Managerial Skills, whereas Micromanagement, Counterproductive Work Patterns, and Counterproductive Communication Styles emerged as the most frequent discouraged practices. We organized all practices into a conceptual map. Conclusion: The findings indicate that software leadership is mainly associated with managerial and interpersonal practices rather than technical expertise. The resulting conceptual map summarizes these practices and can serve as a reference for understanding leadership in software development contexts.

</details>


### [145] [Integrating Log-Based Security Analytics in Agile Workflows: A Real-World Experience Report](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00352)
*Arpit Thool,Chris Brown*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 这是一篇经验报告，描述了"Red Flag Project"中跨职能团队将日志欺诈检测系统集成到敏捷工作流的案例研究。通过半结构化访谈探讨了开发者对该系统的接受意愿、面临的挑战以及对日常开发活动和安全感知的影响，为安全实践与敏捷开发流程的融合提供了实践指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代组织依赖日志数据和监控信号来防护账户劫持和滥用，但将安全分析整合到快速迭代的敏捷工作流中仍面临挑战。

Method: 跨职能团队（8人，含1位作者）每周迭代实现概念验证的日志欺诈检测系统，通过半结构化访谈调查开发者对日志欺诈检测集成的感知情况。

Result: 发现了将安全分析嵌入敏捷工作流的关键经验教训、缓解技术和最佳实践。

Conclusion: 为实践者和研究者提供将安全实践融入现代开发流程的见解，同时保持软件交付的速度和韧性。

Abstract: Modern organizations increasingly rely on log data and monitoring signals to protect products against account takeovers and abuse, yet integrating security analytics into fast-moving Agile workflows remains challenging. While it is important to understand how security practices are developed and sustained within Agile, real-world case studies of such integrations remain scarce. This experience report provides insights on developer perceptions of an effort to integrate log-based fraud detection within an organization, known as the "Red Flag Project". A cross-functional team of eight members (including one author) iterated weekly to implement a proof-of-concept log-based system that alerts stakeholders when accounts exhibit suspicious activity patterns. Through semi-structured interviews, we investigate developer perceptions of log-based fraud detection integration-exploring their willingness to adopt the system, challenges encountered, and the overall impact on day-to-day development activities and security perceptions. Our findings highlight key lessons, mitigation techniques, and best practices for embedding security analytics into Agile workflows. We provide insights for practitioners and researchers seeking to incorporate security practices into modern development processes while maintaining both speed and resilience in software delivery.

</details>


### [146] [Social Bias in LLM-Generated Code: Benchmark and Mitigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00382)
*Fazle Rabbi,Lin Ling,Song Wang,Jinqiu Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究首次系统性地揭示了LLM代码生成中存在严重的人口统计学偏见，发现传统干预方法会加剧而非缓解问题，并提出Fairness Monitor Agent（FMA）作为即插即用的解决方案，有效降低65.1%的偏见并提升功能正确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在需要人口统计学公平性的人类中心应用中生成代码时，现有的评估几乎只关注功能正确性，而忽视社会偏见问题，这是一个重要的研究空白。

Method: 研究采用SocialBias-Bench基准测试（含343个真实编码任务，覆盖7个人口统计学维度），对4个主流LLM进行评估；测试了标准提示级干预措施（思维链推理、公平角色分配）和结构化多智能体框架；提出FMA模块，通过分析任务描述确定需考虑/限制的属性，通过迭代审查过程检测并纠正违规行为。

Result: 1）所有模型均存在严重偏见，代码偏见分数高达60.58%；2）标准提示级干预反而会放大偏见；3）结构化管道在早期角色正确界定范围时可减少偏见；4）向所有角色添加明确的公平指令反而产生更差的结果；5）FMA相比单独的开发智能体将偏见降低65.1%，同时将功能正确率从75.80%提升至83.97%。

Conclusion: 该研究提出Fairness Monitor Agent (FMA)作为即插即用的模块，成功将偏见降低65.1%并将功能正确率从75.80%提升至83.97%，超越了所有其他研究方法，为LLM代码生成中的公平性问题提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed to generate code for human-centered applications where demographic fairness is critical. However, existing evaluations focus almost exclusively on functional correctness, leaving social bias in LLM-generated code largely unexamined. Extending our prior work on Solar, we conduct a comprehensive empirical study using SocialBias-Bench, a benchmark of 343 real-world coding tasks spanning seven demographic dimensions. We evaluate four prominent LLMs and find severe bias across all models, with Code Bias Scores reaching up to 60.58%. We further show that standard prompt-level interventions, such as Chain-of-Thought reasoning and fairness persona assignment, inadvertently amplify bias rather than reduce it. We then investigate whether structured multi-agent software process frameworks can improve fairness, finding that structured pipelines reduce bias when early roles correctly scope what the code should and should not consider. However, adding explicit fairness instructions to all agent roles produces worse outcomes than providing none, suggesting that diffused responsibility goes unaddressed. To address these limitations, we propose the Fairness Monitor Agent (FMA), a modular component that plugs into any existing code generation pipeline without modifying it. FMA analyzes the task description to determine which attributes should be considered or restricted, then detects and corrects violations through an iterative review process, without requiring an executable test suite. Evaluated on all 343 tasks, FMA reduces bias by 65.1% compared to a developer agent alone and improves functional correctness from 75.80% to 83.97%, outperforming all other studied approaches.

</details>


### [147] [ClozeMaster: Fuzzing Rust Compiler by Harnessing LLMs for Infilling Masked Real Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00413)
*Hongyan Gao,Yibiao Yang,Maolin Sun,Jiangchang Wu,Yuming Zhou,Baowen Xu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出clozeMask策略，利用LLM辅助生成Rust编译器测试用例。通过对历史issue报告中的代码进行结构化掩码和LLM填充，在保持bug触发能力的同时提升生成效率。原型工具CLOZEMASTER成功发现27个已确认bug，10个已被修复，并在覆盖率和有效性上超越现有fuzzer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Rust语言因强调内存和线程安全而在关键系统开发中应用日益广泛，但其复杂的语法和严格的编译要求使得为Rust编译器生成有效测试程序面临重大挑战。直接使用LLM生成Rust程序往往产生大量无效测试用例，而历史bug触发的测试用例对软件测试具有重要价值。

Method: 提出了基于括号结构的掩码填充策略clozeMask：首先从历史issue报告中提取测试代码，识别并掩码具有特定结构的代码片段，然后利用LLM对掩码部分进行填充以合成新的测试程序。该方法在保持触发编译器bug能力的同时，发挥LLM的生成能力。

Result: CLOZEMASTER共发现并确认了27个编译器bug（涉及rustc和mrustc），其中10个已被开发者修复。相比现有fuzzer工具，该方法在代码覆盖率和bug检测有效性方面表现更优。

Conclusion: CLOZEMASTER成功识别了27个已确认的rustc和mrustc编译器bug，其中10个已被开发者修复。实验结果表明，该方法在代码覆盖率和测试有效性方面均优于现有fuzzer工具，验证了基于clozeMask策略的LLM辅助编译器测试方法的有效性。

Abstract: Ensuring the reliability of the Rust compiler is of paramount importance, given increasing adoption of Rust for critical systems development, due to its emphasis on memory and thread safety. However, generating valid test programs for the Rust compiler poses significant challenges, given Rust's complex syntax and strict requirements. With the growing popularity of large language models (LLMs), much research in software testing has explored using LLMs to generate test cases. Still, directly using LLMs to generate Rust programs often results in a large number of invalid test cases. Existing studies have indicated that test cases triggering historical compiler bugs can assist in software testing. Our investigation into Rust compiler bug issues supports this observation. Inspired by existing work and our empirical research, we introduce a bracket-based masking and filling strategy called clozeMask. The clozeMask strategy involves extracting test code from historical issue reports, identifying and masking code snippets with specific structures, and using an LLM to fill in the masked portions for synthesizing new test programs. This approach harnesses the generative capabilities of LLMs while retaining the ability to trigger Rust compiler bugs. It enables comprehensive testing of the compiler's behavior, particularly exploring edge cases. We implemented our approach as a prototype CLOZEMASTER. CLOZEMASTER has identified 27 confirmed bugs for rustc and mrustc, of which 10 have been fixed by developers. Furthermore, our experimental results indicate that CLOZEMASTER outperforms existing fuzzers in terms of code coverage and effectiveness.

</details>


### [148] [Improving LLM Code Generation via Requirement-Aware Curriculum Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00433)
*Shouyu Yin,Zhao Tian,Junjie Chen,Shikai Guo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出RECRL框架，通过要求感知、优化和自适应采样三个核心组件解决现有课程强化学习方法在代码生成任务中的局限性，实验证明其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着编程需求复杂度增加，现有LLM在代码生成任务上存在明显性能瓶颈。现有CRL方法存在三大缺陷：要求难度感知不对齐、缺乏难度优化机制、采样策略次优。

Method: 提出RECRL框架，包含：(1)模型特定要求难度自动感知机制；(2)挑战性要求优化模块以提升训练数据利用效率；(3)自适应课程采样策略，生成难度平滑过渡的训练批次。

Result: 在5个主流LLM和5个代码生成基准数据集上，相比5个SOTA基线方法，RECRL实现平均Pass@1提升1.23%-5.62%。

Conclusion: RECRL通过要求难度感知、优化和自适应采样三方面创新，显著提升LLM代码生成性能，平均Pass@1提升1.23%-5.62%，证明了要求工程视角在强化学习训练中的重要价值。

Abstract: Code generation, which aims to automatically generate source code from given programming requirements, has the potential to substantially improve software development efficiency. With the rapid advancement of large language models (LLMs), LLM-based code generation has attracted widespread attention from both academia and industry. However, as programming requirements become increasingly complex, existing LLMs still exhibit notable performance limitations. To address this challenge, recent studies have proposed training-based curriculum reinforcement learning (CRL) strategies to improve LLM code generation performance. Despite their effectiveness, existing CRL approaches suffer from several limitations, including misaligned requirement difficulty perception, the absence of requirement difficulty optimization, and suboptimal curriculum sampling strategies. In CRL-based code generation, programming requirements serve as the sole input to the model, making their quality and difficulty critical to training effectiveness. Motivated by insights from software requirements engineering, we propose RECRL, a novel requirement-aware curriculum reinforcement learning framework for enhancing LLM-based code generation. RECRL automatically perceives model-specific requirement difficulty, optimizes challenging requirements to improve training data utilization, and employs an adaptive curriculum sampling strategy to construct training batches with smoothly varying difficulty. Extensive experiments on five state-of-the-art LLMs across five widely-used code generation benchmarks by comparing with five state-of-the-art baselines, demonstrate the significant effectiveness of RECRL. For example, RECRL achieves an average Pass@1 improvement of 1.23%-5.62% over all state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [149] [Q-ARE: An Evaluation Dataset for Query Based API Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00472)
*Shenglong Wu,Xunhui Zhang,Tao Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文构建了Q-ARE评估数据集，系统评估了现有查询式API推荐方法和大语言模型的表现，发现随着API调用深度增加和调用密度降低，方法性能显著下降，表明现有方法仍难以处理多层级方法调用结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: As software systems scale up, developers face increasing difficulty in selecting appropriate APIs from numerous options. Efficiently identifying APIs that satisfy functional requirements has become a key challenge. The semantic understanding capabilities of existing query-based API recommendation methods need evaluation.

Method: 1) Construct Q-ARE dataset from GitHub Java projects; 2) Analyze methods and invocation chains to identify third-party APIs through recursive multi-level expansion; 3) Introduce two evaluation metrics: API Call Depth (invocation distance) and Invocation Density (proportion of code lines associated with target API); 4) Systematically evaluate query-based API recommendation methods and general LLMs.

Result: Performance of existing methods drops significantly as API Call Depth increases and invocation density decreases, indicating that current query-based API recommendation methods and general LLMs still struggle with multi-level method invocation structures.

Conclusion: Q-ARE provides a new benchmark for assessing semantic understanding in API recommendation, revealing that existing methods struggle with multi-level method invocation structures. The dataset and metrics (API Call Depth, Invocation Density) offer insights for improving future algorithms.

Abstract: As software systems grow in scale, developers face increasing difficulty in selecting appropriate Application Programming Interfaces (APIs) from numerous options. Efficiently identifying APIs that satisfy functional requirements has become a key challenge. To evaluate the semantic understanding of existing query-based API recommendation methods, this paper constructs Q-ARE (Query-based API Recommendation Evaluation), a dataset based on open-source Java projects from GitHub. Methods and their invocation chains are analyzed to identify third-party APIs directly or indirectly invoked by target methods, recursively expanding multi-level invocations to unify hierarchical call structures into API recommendation target sets. Furthermore, we introduce two metrics: API Call Depth, measuring the invocation distance between a query method and a target API, and Invocation Density, quantifying the proportion of code lines associated with the target API in the invocation chain. Based on Q-ARE, we systematically evaluate several query-based API recommendation methods and general Large Language Models (LLMs). Results show that performance drops significantly as API Call Depth increases and invocation density decreases, indicating that existing methods still struggle with multi-level method invocation structures. Q-ARE and its metrics provide a new benchmark for assessing semantic understanding in API recommendation and offer insights for improving future algorithms.

</details>


### [150] [EnCoDe: Energy Estimation of Source Code At Design-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00504)
*Shailender Goyal,Akhila Matathammal,Karthik Vaidhyanathan*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出EnCoDe方法论，通过PowerLens实现亚毫秒级能量测量，基于18,000+程序建立精细粒度数据集，并利用机器学习模型实现设计时代码块级能耗预测，R²达0.75，分类准确率80.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具仅在运行时以粗粒度（进程或方法级别）测量能耗，无法揭示小代码块（如函数、循环、条件语句）对能耗的具体贡献，阻碍了开发者在设计时推理和比较编程结构的能效。

Method: (1) PowerLens: 亚毫秒级精细能量测量方法；(2) 基于18,000+ Python程序的代码块进行实证研究，探索静态代码特征（结构、复杂度、密度、上下文特征）与能耗的线性和非线性关系；(3) 基于机器学习的预测模型，利用这些特征在设计时估计和分类代码块级能耗。

Result: 回归模型 R² = 0.75，分类模型准确率 80.6%，可稳定、可重复地识别能耗热点。

Conclusion: EnCoDe enables developers to localize and address inefficient code regions early in the development process without execution, bridging the gap between design-time code analysis and runtime energy consumption.

Abstract: Energy efficiency has emerged as a vital attribute of software quality, with significant implications for both environmental sustainability and operational costs. However, existing profiling tools operate only at runtime and coarse granularity, typically capturing energy at the process or method level. Such tools fail to expose how small code blocks, such as functions, loops, and conditionals, contribute to energy consumption, preventing developers from reasoning about and comparing the energy efficiency of programming constructs during design-time. To address this gap, we propose EnCoDe, a methodology for fine-grained, design-time energy estimation, with the following key contributions: (1) PowerLens, a novel measurement methodology that achieves reliable sub-millisecond energy readings for small code blocks; (2) Extensive empirical study on code blocks extracted from over 18,000 Python programs, uncovering linear and non-linear relationships between energy consumption and static code features such as structural, complexity, density, and contextual characteristics, resulting in a first-of-its-kind fine-grained dataset; and (3) Predictive modeling, in which machine learning models are trained on these features to accurately estimate and classify block-level energy consumption at design-time. Our results demonstrate stable, reproducible block-level estimations, with regressors achieving R^2 = 0.75 and classifiers achieving 80.6% accuracy in identifying energy hotspots, enabling developers to localize and address inefficient code regions early in the development process without execution.

</details>


### [151] [From Research to Practice: An Interactive Rapid Review of Autonomous Driving System Testing in Industry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00531)
*Qunying Song,Ali Nouri,Håkan Sivencrona,Federica Sarro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过与21名汽车行业从业者合作开展互动式快速综述，识别了自动驾驶系统测试中的12个关键挑战，并将端到端ADS测试的方法与完整性确定为最关键问题；通过对17项相关研究的分析，揭示了当前ADS测试研究在工业适用性方面的不足，强调需要开发更具场景感知能力且符合行业需求的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统虽已日益广泛部署，但测试仍面临开放环境、复杂场景以及缺乏成熟流程和指标的挑战；学术界的研究与工业实践之间存在显著差距。

Method: 与一家领先汽车企业的21名从业者合作开展互动式快速综述，识别出12个关键挑战，并对与两个最关键挑战相关的17项研究研究进行分析，评估其与实践的相关性和适用性。

Result: 识别出ADS测试的12个关键挑战，其中最关键的2个涉及端到端ADS测试的方法和完整性；现有研究多聚焦于生成关键测试场景。

Conclusion: 迫切需要更多具有场景感知能力的、面向工业实际需求的解决方案，以弥合研究与实践之间的差距；当前研究主要关注生成关键测试场景，但与工业实践的相关性和适用性仍有待提升。

Abstract: Autonomous driving systems (ADS) are increasingly deployed in real traffic, yet testing remains fundamentally challenging due to open environments, complex scenarios, and the lack of established processes and metrics. Despite extensive research, a gap persists between academic advances and their applicability in industrial practice. To address this, we conduct an interactive rapid review in collaboration with 21 practitioners from a leading automotive company. Practitioners identified 12 key challenges in ADS testing, and prioritised two as the most critical issues, namely approaches to and completeness of testing for End-to-End (E2E) ADS. We analyzed 17 research studies relevant to these two challenges, most of which focus on generating critical testing scenarios, and subsequently assessed their relevance and applicability in practice. Our study provides the first practitioner-driven review and evaluation of current ADS testing research, reveals practical challenges in ADS testing, offers rapid insights for practitioners, and highlights the need for more context-aware, industry-relevant solutions to bridge the gap between research and practice.

</details>


### [152] [Themis: Training Robust Multilingual Code Reward Models for Flexible Multi-Criteria Scoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00754)
*Indraneil Paul,Glavaš Glavas,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究系统性地探讨了多语言、多标准代码奖励模型的训练与评估，构建了大规模偏好数据集Themis-CodePreference（35万+偏好对），并基于此训练了600M-32B参数的Themis-RM模型族，证明了多标准训练和跨语言迁移对代码奖励建模的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码奖励模型研究主要聚焦于执行反馈（功能正确性），这限制了后训练只能优化自包含可执行代码的语义正确性，缺乏对代码质量、效率、可读性等多维度的评价能力。

Method: 1）构建Themis-CodeRewardBench基准，在5个偏好维度和8种编程语言上评测50+代码/数学/通用奖励模型；2）开发Themis-CodePreference数据集（35万+偏好对），规模为目前开源之最；3）基于该数据集训练Themis-RM系列模型（600M-32B参数），实现多标准、多语言的代码奖励评分。

Result: 1）当前奖励模型在功能正确性之外的能力有限；2）模型呈现正向Scaling趋势；3）跨语言迁移效果显著；4）多标准训练对可靠的代码奖励建模至关重要。

Conclusion: 多标准代码奖励模型（Themis-RM）可有效实现灵活的多维度评分，跨语言迁移能力强，模型规模从600M到32B参数不等，为代码生成的后训练提供了更可靠的评价工具。

Abstract: Reward models (RMs) have become an indispensable fixture of the language model (LM) post-training playbook, enabling policy alignment and test-time scaling. Research on the application of RMs in code generation, however, has been comparatively sparse, with existing work largely focusing on execution feedback. This choice constrains post-training to optimizing functional correctness over self-contained executable code. In this work, we examine the training and evaluation of multilingual, multi-criteria code RMs. To this end, we first compile Themis-CodeRewardBench, a benchmark to evaluate code RMs across five preference dimensions (i.e., criteria) and eight programming languages, on which we profile 50+ code, math, and general-purpose RMs. Observing the limited proficiency of current RMs beyond scoring for functional correctness, we develop Themis-CodePreference, the largest open-source collection of code preferences to date (more than 350k preference pairs), and use it to train Themis-RM, a suite of multilingual code reward models for flexible multi-criteria scoring, ranging in size from 600M to 32B parameters. Our experiments and ablations demonstrate positive scaling trends, strong cross-lingual transfer when training on diverse preferences, and the importance of multi-criteria training for reliable code reward modeling.

</details>


### [153] [GeoContra: From Fluent GIS Code to Verifiable Spatial Analysis with Geography-Grounded Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00782)
*Yinhao Xiao,Rongbo Xiao,Yihan Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: GeoContra是一个用于LLM驱动的Python GIS工作流的验证与修复框架，通过地理空间契约、静态检查、运行时验证和语义验证的组合，显著提升空间分析正确率至77.5-81.5%，平均提升26.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GIScience中的可靠空间分析需要保持坐标语义、拓扑、单位定义和地理可信度。当前基于LLM的GIS系统能够生成流畅的脚本，但很少在大规模场景下强制执行这些地理规则。

Method: 提出GeoContra框架，将每个任务表示为包含自然语言问题、模式、CRS元数据、期望输出、空间谓词、拓扑结构、度量、操作和禁止快捷方式的可执行地理空间契约。生成的程序经历静态规则检查、运行时验证和语义验证，违规项被反馈到有界修复循环中。

Result: 在15个波士顿地区区域、9个任务族和11个开源模型的7079个真实地理空间任务上评估，GeoContra将DeepSeek-V4的空间正确率从47.6%提升至77.5%，Kimi-K2.5从57.7%提升至81.5%。11个开源模型的平均正确率提升26.6%。

Conclusion: GeoContra将流畅的代码生成转化为可验证的空间分析，能够捕获负行程时间、CRS/字段模式违规、缺失谓词和脆弱的输出转换等问题，从而产生可执行但地理上无效的结果。该框架为LLM驱动的GIS工作流提供了可靠的验证和修复能力。

Abstract: Reliable spatial analysis in GIScience requires preserving coordinate semantics, topology, units, and geographic plausibility. Current LLM-based GIS systems generate fluent scripts but rarely enforce these geographic rules at scale. We present GeoContra, a verification and repair framework for LLM-driven Python GIS workflows. It represents each task as an executable geospatial contract-including natural-language questions, schemas, CRS metadata, expected outputs, spatial predicates, topology, metrics, required operations, and forbidden shortcuts. Generated programs undergo static rule inspection, runtime validation, and semantic verification, with violations fed back into a bounded repair loop. Evaluated on 7,079 real geospatial tasks across 15 Boston-area zones, 9 task families, and 11 open-source models (600 runs each), GeoContra improves spatial correctness on closed models from 47.6% to 77.5% for DeepSeek-V4 and from 57.7% to 81.5% for Kimi-K2.5. Across 11 open models, average correctness rises by 26.6%. GeoContra turns fluent code production into verifiable spatial analysis, catching negative travel times, CRS/field-schema violations, missing predicates, and brittle output casts that otherwise yield executable but geographically invalid results.

</details>


### [154] [Can Coding Agents Reproduce Findings in Computational Materials Science?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00803)
*Ziyang Huang,Yi Cao,Ali K. Shargh,Jing Luo,Ruidong Mei,Mohd Zaki,Zhan Liu,Wyatt Bunstine,William Jurayj,Somdatta Goswami,Tyrel McQueen,Michael Shields,Jaafar El-Awady,Paulette Clancy,Benjamin Van Durme,Nicholas Andrews,William Walden,Daniel Khashabi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AutoMat是一个评估LLM智能体在计算材料科学领域复现科学声明能力的基准测试，涵盖三个核心挑战：从不足规范中恢复计算程序、导航专业工具链、判断证据是否支撑声明。研究发现当前LLM智能体在该任务上成功率较低（最佳仅54.1%），主要受限于程序恢复不完整、方法偏离和执行不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在软件工程基准测试上表现优异，但在计算科学工作流中的适用性尚不明确。这些工作流不仅需要强编码能力，还需要复杂领域特定程序的导航能力以及在科学声明背景下解释结果的能力。

Method: 1) 与领域专家紧密合作，从真实材料科学论文中精选声明；2) 测试编码智能体是否能恢复并执行支撑（或推翻）这些声明的端到端工作流；3) 跨多个基础模型评估多种代表性编码智能体设置。

Result: 当前LLM智能体在AutoMat上整体成功率较低，最佳设置仅达到54.1%。错误分析揭示：智能体在需从论文文本独立重建工作流时表现最差；主要失败原因包括：程序不完整、方法偏差和执行脆弱性。

Conclusion: AutoMat作为计算科学可重复性基准测试和诊断AI-for-science智能体系统当前局限性的工具，具有重要价值。当前LLM智能体在计算科学工作流中表现不佳，主要受限于程序不完整、方法偏差和执行脆弱性。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as autonomous coding agents and have achieved remarkably strong performance on software engineering benchmarks. However, it is unclear whether such success transfers to computational scientific workflows, where tasks require not only strong coding ability, but also the ability to navigate complex, domain-specific procedures and to interpret results in the context of scientific claims. To address this question, we present AutoMat, a benchmark for evaluating LLM-based agents' ability to reproduce claims from computational materials science. AutoMat poses three interrelated challenges: recovering underspecified computational procedures, navigating specialized toolchains, and determining whether the resulting evidence supports a claim. By working closely with subject matter experts, we curate a set of claims from real materials science papers to test whether coding agents can recover and execute the end-to-end workflow needed to support (or undermine) such claims. We then evaluate multiple representative coding agent settings across several foundation models. Our results show that current LLM-based agents obtain low overall success rates on AutoMat, with the best-performing setting achieving a success rate of only 54.1%. Error analysis further reveals that agents perform worst when workflows must be reconstructed from paper text alone and that they fail primarily due to incomplete procedures, methodological deviations, and execution fragility. Taken together, these findings position AutoMat as both a benchmark for computational scientific reproducibility and a tool for diagnosing the current limitations of agentic systems in AI-for-science settings.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [Minimal, Local, Causal Explanations for Jailbreak Success in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00123)
*Shubham Kumar,Narendra Ahuja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LOCA provides local, causal explanations for why specific jailbreaks succeed in bypassing LLM safety measures by identifying minimal interpretable representation changes that causally trigger refusal. It outperforms prior global explanation methods, requiring only ~6 changes to induce refusal compared to prior methods' failure after 20 changes.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current safety-trained LLMs remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks, yet we lack robust understanding of why. Prior work attempts global explanations (e.g., all jailbreaks reduce harmfulness), but fails to capture that different jailbreak strategies succeed through different mechanisms and that the same jailbreak may not work across different harmful request categories (violence vs. cyberattack). As frontier models increasingly operate autonomously in high-stakes settings, understanding these vulnerabilities becomes critical.

Method: LOCA identifies minimal sets of interpretable intermediate representation changes that causally induce model refusal on successful jailbreak requests. It treats explanation generation as a causal intervention problem, testing which representation directions, when modified, cause the model to refuse. Evaluation conducted on Gemma and Llama chat models against a large jailbreak benchmark.

Result: LOCA successfully induces refusal with approximately 6 interpretable changes on average. Prior methods fail to achieve refusal even after 20 changes. The method outperforms prior approaches adapted to this local explanation setting across Gemma and Llama chat models on diverse harmful request categories.

Conclusion: LOCA represents a significant advancement in explaining jailbreak susceptibility in LLMs by providing local, causal explanations rather than global ones. It successfully demonstrates that minimal interpretable representation changes (averaging 6) can induce model refusal, significantly outperforming prior methods that fail with 20+ changes. This work contributes to safer deployment of frontier models in high-stakes autonomous settings.

Abstract: Safety trained large language models (LLMs) can often be induced to answer harmful requests through jailbreak prompts. Because we lack a robust understanding of why LLMs are susceptible to jailbreaks, future frontier models operating more autonomously in higher-stakes settings may similarly be vulnerable to such attacks. Prior work has studied jailbreak success by examining the model's intermediate representations, identifying directions in this space that causally encode concepts like harmfulness and refusal. Then, they globally explain all jailbreak attacks as attempting to reduce or strengthen these concepts (e.g., reduce harmfulness). However, different jailbreak strategies may succeed by strengthening or suppressing different intermediate concepts, and the same jailbreak strategy may not work for different harmful request categories (e.g., violence vs. cyberattack); thus, we seek to give a local explanation -- i.e., why did this specific jailbreak succeed? To address this gap, we introduce LOCA, a method that gives Local, CAusal explanations of jailbreak success by identifying a minimal set of interpretable, intermediate representation changes that causally induce model refusal on an otherwise successful jailbreak request. We evaluate LOCA on harmful original-jailbreak pairs from a large jailbreak benchmark across Gemma and Llama chat models, comparing against prior methods adapted to this setting. LOCA can successfully induce refusal by making, on average, six interpretable changes; prior work routinely fails to achieve refusal even after 20 changes. LOCA is a step toward mechanistic, local explanations of jailbreak success in LLMs. Code to be released.

</details>


### [156] [Are Tools All We Need? Unveiling the Tool-Use Tax in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00136)
*Kaituo Zhang,Zhen Xiong,Mingyu Zhong,Zhimeng Jiang,Zhouyuan Yuan,Zhecheng Li,Ying Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文揭示了工具增强推理的一个关键局限：在存在语义干扰的情况下，工具调用协议本身的额外开销（"工具使用税"）可能导致收益为负。通过因素分解框架和G-STEP门控机制的实验验证，说明轻量级修复不足以根本解决问题，提升模型内在推理和工具交互能力才是关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学界普遍认为工具增强推理能够提升大语言模型的推理能力和可靠性，但作者发现这一共识在语义干扰存在时并不成立。

Method: 采用因素分解干预框架（Factorized Intervention Framework），将提示格式化成本、工具调用协议开销和工具执行收益三者解耦，分别评估各因素对性能的影响。

Result: 在语义噪声环境下，工具带来的收益往往无法抵消"工具使用税"——即工具调用协议本身引入的性能下降。

Conclusion: G-STEP作为一种轻量级推理时门控机制能够部分缓解工具调用协议带来的误差，但更根本的改进需要提升模型自身的推理能力和工具交互能力。工具增强推理的优势并非无条件成立，在语义噪声环境下需谨慎评估其实际效益。

Abstract: Tool-augmented reasoning has become a popular direction for LLM-based agents, and it is widely assumed to improve reasoning and reliability. However, we demonstrate that this consensus does not always hold: in the presence of semantic distractors, tool-augmented reasoning does not necessarily outperform native CoT. To explain this performance gap, we propose a Factorized Intervention Framework that isolates the cost of prompt formatting, the overhead of the tool-calling protocol, and the actual gain from executing tools. Our analysis reveals a critical tradeoff: under semantic noise, the gains from tools often fail to offset the "tool-use tax", which is the performance degradation introduced by the tool-calling protocol itself. To address this, we introduce G-STEP, a lightweight inference-time gate to mitigate protocol-induced errors. While this yields partial recovery, our findings suggest that more substantial improvements still require strengthening the model's intrinsic reasoning and tool-interaction capabilities.

</details>


### [157] [TUR-DPO: Topology- and Uncertainty-Aware Direct Preference Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00224)
*Abdulhady Abas Abdullah,Fatemeh Daneshfar,Seyedali Mirjalili,Mourad Oussalah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TUR-DPO是一种考虑推理拓扑和质量的不确定性感知DPO变体，通过将推理过程质量纳入奖励信号，解决了传统DPO仅关注结果胜负而忽视推导过程的问题，在多种任务上超越DPO并匹敌PPO性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流的对齐方法DPO将偏好视为简单的胜负信号，对推理过程中产生的噪声或脆弱偏好信号敏感，无法捕捉答案推导过程的质量。

Method: 通过生成轻量级推理拓扑，结合语义忠实度、实用性和拓扑质量形成一个可学习的校准不确定性信号，并将其因子化为可学习的奖励权重，融入不确定性加权的DPO目标，仅依赖固定或移动的参考策略，无需强化学习或在线采样。

Result: 在7-8B开源模型上，数学推理、事实问答、摘要、帮助性/无害性对话等多个基准测试中，显著提升了裁判胜率、语义忠实度和校准性能；在多模态和长上下文场景中也观察到一致性提升。

Conclusion: TUR-DPO是一种无强化学习的轻量级方法，在推理任务上匹配或超越PPO性能，同时保持操作简单性，无需在线rollout。适用于多模态和长上下文场景。

Abstract: Aligning large language models (LLMs) with human preferences is commonly done via reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) with Proximal Policy Optimization (PPO) or, more simply, via Direct Preference Optimization (DPO). While DPO is stable and RL-free, it treats preferences as flat winner vs. loser signals and is sensitive to noisy or brittle preferences arising from fragile chains of thought. We propose TUR-DPO, a topology- and uncertainty-aware variant of DPO that rewards how answers are derived, not only what they say, by eliciting lightweight reasoning topologies and combining semantic faithfulness, utility, and topology quality into a calibrated uncertainty signal. A small learnable reward is factorized over these signals and incorporated into an uncertainty-weighted DPO objective that remains RL-free and relies only on a fixed or moving reference policy. Empirically, across open 7-8B models and benchmarks spanning mathematical reasoning, factual question answering, summarization, and helpful/harmless dialogue, TUR-DPO improves judge win-rates, faithfulness, and calibration relative to DPO while preserving training simplicity and avoiding online rollouts. We further observe consistent gains in multimodal and long-context settings, and show that TUR-DPO matches or exceeds PPO on reasoning-centric tasks while maintaining operational simplicity.

</details>


### [158] [ARMOR 2025: A Military-Aligned Benchmark for Evaluating Large Language Model Safety Beyond Civilian Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00245)
*Sydney Johns,Heng Jin,Chaoyu Zhang,Y. Thomas Hou,Wenjing Lou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出ARMOR 2025军事对齐安全基准，通过12分类法、519个基于三大军事 doctrine 的问题评估21个商业LLM，揭示现有LLM在军事应用中的安全对齐关键缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全基准仅关注一般社会风险，缺乏针对军事行动法律合规性的评估方法；而LLM在军事防御领域应用需求日益增长，亟需符合军事 doctrine 标准的安全评估体系。

Method: 提出了ARMOR 2025基准测试，从法律战争、交战规则和联合伦理法规三大核心军事 doctrine提取源文本，生成保留规则原意的多项选择题，并通过OODA决策框架组织12类分类法，系统测试21个商业LLM的准确率和拒答能力。

Result: ARMOR 2025包含519个基于 doctrine 的提示词，经21个商业LLM评估，结果显示这些模型在军事应用安全对齐方面存在显著缺陷。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前LLM在军事应用安全对齐方面的关键不足，证明了对军事特定安全基准的迫切需求。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are now being explored for defense applications that require reliable and legally compliant decision support. They also hold significant potential to enhance decision making, coordination, and operational efficiency in military contexts. These uses demand evaluation methods that reflect the doctrinal standards that guide real military operations. Existing safety benchmarks focus on general social risks and do not test whether models follow the legal and ethical rules that govern real military operations. To address this gap, we introduce ARMOR 2025, a military aligned safety benchmark grounded in three core military doctrines the Law of War, the Rules of Engagement, and the Joint Ethics Regulation. We extract doctrinal text from these sources and generate multiple choice questions that preserve the intended meaning of each rule. The benchmark is organized through a taxonomy informed by the Observe Orient Decide Act (OODA) decision making framework. This structure enables systematic testing of accuracy and refusal across military relevant decision types. This benchmark features a structured 12-category taxonomy, 519 doctrinally grounded prompts, and rigorous evaluation procedures applied to 21 commercial LLMs. Evaluation results reveal critical gaps in safety alignment for military applications.

</details>


### [159] [Agentic AI for Trip Planning Optimization Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00276)
*Tiejin Chen,Ahmadreza Moradipari,Kyungtae Han,Hua Wei,Nejib Ammar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This paper addresses limitations in intelligent vehicle trip planning by proposing an agentic AI framework with orchestrated multi-agent coordination (for traffic, charging, and POI) and a new benchmark dataset (TOP) with ground truth optimal solutions. The system achieves 77.4% accuracy, outperforming single-agent and workflow-based baselines.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing trip planning systems focus on feasibility rather than optimization, and current benchmarks lack ground truth answers, preventing objective evaluation of optimization performance.

Method: Proposed an agentic AI framework with an orchestration agent coordinating specialized agents (traffic, charging, and POI) for dynamic refinement, along with the Trip-planning Optimization Problems (TOP) Dataset providing ground truth optimal solutions.

Result: Achieved 77.4% accuracy on the TOP Benchmark, significantly outperforming baseline methods.

Conclusion: The agentic framework with orchestrated multi-agent reasoning significantly outperforms both single-agent and workflow-based multi-agent baselines, demonstrating the importance of coordinated agentic reasoning for robust trip planning optimization.

Abstract: Trip planning for intelligent vehicles increasingly requires selecting optimal routes rather than merely producing feasible itineraries, as interacting factors such as travel time, energy consumption, and traffic conditions directly affect plan quality. Yet existing systems are largely designed for feasibility-oriented planning, and current benchmarks provide only reference answers without ground truth, preventing objective evaluation of optimization performance. In our paper, we address these limitations with an agentic AI framework that enables dynamic refinement through an orchestration agent coordinating specialized agents for traffic, charging, and points of interest, and with the Trip-planning Optimization Problems Dataset, which supplies definitive optimal solutions and category-level task structure for fine-grained analysis. Experiments show that our system achieves 77.4\% accuracy on the TOP Benchmark, significantly outperforming single-agent and workflow-based multi-agent baselines, demonstrating the importance of orchestrated agentic reasoning for robust trip planning optimization.

</details>


### [160] [Token Arena: A Continuous Benchmark Unifying Energy and Cognition in AI Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00300)
*Yuxuan Gao,Megan Wang,Yi Ling Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TokenArena是一个连续基准测试框架，在端点粒度上评估AI推理性能，通过5个核心维度和3个复合指标（焦耳/正确答案、美元/正确答案、端点保真度）揭示了78个端点间的显著差异，证明同一模型在不同端点表现差异巨大，且工作负载特征会显著影响排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公共推理基准测试仅在模型和提供商层面比较AI系统，但实际部署决策的粒度是端点——即特定量化、解码策略、区域和服务栈下的(提供商、模型、库存单位)元组。缺乏针对端点级别的精细评估工具。

Method: TokenArena对78个端点、12个模型族进行连续基准测试，从五个核心维度（输出速度、首个token时间、工作负载混合价格、有效上下文长度、实时端点质量）进行评估，并结合建模能耗估算，综合生成三个复合指标：每次正确答案的焦耳数、每次正确答案的美元成本、端点保真度（与第一方参考的输出分布相似性）。

Result: 研究发现：同一模型在不同端点上的平均准确率差异可达12.5个百分点（数学和代码任务），与第一方的指纹相似性差异达12个百分点，尾延迟差异一个数量级，建模的每次正确答案焦耳数差异达6.2倍。工作负载感知的混合定价显著重排排行榜：聊天预设（3:1输入:输出）下前10名中的7个端点在检索增强预设（20:1）下跌出前10；而推理预设（1:5）则抬高了聊天预设下因价格被惩罚的前沿闭源模型。

Conclusion: TokenArena是一个开放的基准测试框架和方法论，而非单一排名；已发布完整的方法论、schema、探测和评估工具以及v1.0排行榜快照（CC BY 4.0许可），支持外部复现。该研究揭示了AI推理端点层面的显著差异，为实际部署决策提供了更精细的评估依据。

Abstract: Public inference benchmarks compare AI systems at the model and provider level, but the unit at which deployment decisions are actually made is the endpoint: the (provider, model, stock-keeping-unit) tuple at which a specific quantization, decoding strategy, region, and serving stack is exposed. We introduce TokenArena, a continuous benchmark that measures inference at endpoint granularity along five core axes (output speed, time to first token, workload-blended price, effective context, and quality on the live endpoint) and synthesizes them, together with a modeled energy estimate, into three headline composites: joules per correct answer, dollars per correct answer, and endpoint fidelity (output-distribution similarity to a first-party reference). The framework's novelty is empirical and methodological. Across 78 endpoints serving 12 model families, the same model on different endpoints differs in mean accuracy by up to 12.5 points on math and code, in fingerprint similarity to first party by up to 12 points, in tail latency by an order of magnitude, and in modeled joules per correct answer by a factor of 6.2. We further show that workload-aware blended pricing reorders the leaderboard substantially: 7 of 10 top-ranked endpoints under the chat preset (3:1 input:output) fall out of the top 10 under the retrieval-augmented preset (20:1), and the reasoning preset (1:5) elevates frontier closed models that the chat preset penalizes on price. We release the framework, schema, probe and eval harness, and a v1.0 leaderboard snapshot under CC BY 4.0. TokenArena is a methodology, not a single ranking; we publish full provenance and limitations and welcome external replication.

</details>


### [161] [AgentFloor: How Far Up the tool use Ladder Can Small Open-Weight Models Go?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00334)
*Ranit Karmakar,Jayita Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出AgentFloor基准测试评估agent系统中模型规模需求，发现最强开源模型已可匹配GPT-5性能，长期规划仍是差距所在，建议采用分层模型架构处理不同复杂度的agent任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估未直接回答agent工作流中哪些部分真正需要大型前沿模型，哪些可由较小模型处理的生产实际问题。

Method: 构建AgentFloor基准测试（30个任务，六层能力阶梯），涵盖指令遵循、工具使用、多步协调和长期规划；对16个开源模型（0.27B-32B）和GPT-5进行16,542次评分运行评估。

Result: 小型和中型开源模型已能处理大部分短期、结构化的工具使用任务；最强开源模型在整体性能上匹配GPT-5；差距主要体现在需要持续协调和长期约束追踪的长期规划任务；模型失败对针对性干预的响应具有模型特异性而非通用性。

Conclusion: Agentic系统设计的实用原则：使用小型开源模型处理常规任务，仅将大型前沿模型保留用于需要深度规划和控制的狭窄任务类别。同时发现模型规模并非唯一决定因素，部分失败可通过针对性干预解决，但效果因模型而异。

Abstract: Production agentic systems make many model calls per user request, and most of those calls are short, structured, and routine. This raises a practical routing question that existing evaluations do not directly answer: which parts of an agent workflow truly require large frontier intelligence, and which can be handled by smaller models? We introduce AgentFloor, a deterministic 30-task benchmark organized as a six-tier capability ladder, spanning instruction following, tool use, multi-step coordination, and long-horizon planning under persistent constraints. We evaluate 16 open-weight models, from 0.27B to 32B parameters, alongside GPT-5 across 16,542 scored runs. Our results reveal a clear boundary of model necessity. Small and mid-sized open-weight models are already sufficient for much of the short-horizon, structured tool use work that dominates real agent pipelines, and in aggregate, the strongest open-weight model matches GPT-5 on our benchmark while being substantially cheaper and faster to run. The gap appears most clearly on long-horizon planning tasks that require sustained coordination and reliable constraint tracking over many steps, where frontier models still hold an advantage, though neither side reaches strong reliability. We also find that this boundary is not explained by scale alone: some failures respond to targeted interventions, but the effects are model-specific rather than universal. These findings suggest a practical design principle for agentic systems: use smaller open-weight models for the broad base of routine actions, and reserve large frontier models for the narrower class of tasks that truly demand deeper planning and control. We release the benchmark, harness, sweep configurations, and full run corpus.

</details>


### [162] [Physically Native World Models: A Hamiltonian Perspective on Generative World Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00412)
*Sen Cui,Jingheng Ma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"哈密顿世界模型"作为物理驱动的世界建模新视角，通过将观测编码到结构化潜在相空间，利用哈密顿启发的动力学演化状态并进行规划，旨在解决现有世界模型在物理可靠性、动作可控性和长时域稳定性方面的瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前世界模型研究分为三条独立路线：2D视频生成模型、3D场景重建模型和JEPA类潜空间预测模型。这些方法在生成逼真未来方面有进展，但无法提供物理可靠、动作可控、长时域稳定的预测，难以支撑具体化决策智能。

Method: 核心方法：(1)将观测编码为结构化潜在相空间表示；(2)通过哈密顿启发动力学演化潜状态，引入控制项、耗散项和残差项；(3)将预测轨迹解码为未来观测；(4)利用展开轨迹进行规划。

Result: 理论层面：哈密顿结构可提升可解释性、数据效率和长时域稳定性。实践层面：需应对摩擦、接触、非保守力和可变形物体等真实机器人场景挑战。

Conclusion: 哈密顿世界模型为物理驱动的世界建模提供了有前景的框架，但需进一步解决复杂动力学建模问题才能应用于真实机器人系统。

Abstract: World models have recently re-emerged as a central paradigm for embodied intelligence, robotics, autonomous driving, and model-based reinforcement learning. However, current world model research is often dominated by three partially separated routes: 2D video-generative models that emphasize visual future synthesis, 3D scene-centric models that emphasize spatial reconstruction, and JEPA-like latent models that emphasize abstract predictive representations. While each route has made important progress, they still struggle to provide physically reliable, action-controllable, and long-horizon stable predictions for embodied decision making. In this paper, we argue that the bottleneck of world models is no longer only whether they can generate realistic futures, but whether those futures are physically meaningful and useful for action. We propose \emph{Hamiltonian World Models} as a physically grounded perspective on world modeling. The key idea is to encode observations into a structured latent phase space, evolve the latent state through Hamiltonian-inspired dynamics with control, dissipation, and residual terms, decode the predicted trajectory into future observations, and use the resulting rollouts for planning. We discuss how Hamiltonian structure may improve interpretability, data efficiency, and long-horizon stability, while also noting practical challenges in real-world robotic scenes involving friction, contact, non-conservative forces, and deformable objects.

</details>


### [163] [Thinking in Text and Images: Interleaved Vision--Language Reasoning Traces for Long-Horizon Robot Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00438)
*Jinkun Liu,Haohan Chi,Lingfeng Zhang,Yifan Xie,YuAn Wang,Long Chen,Hangjun Ye,Xiaoshuai Hao,Wenbo Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出IVLR框架，通过显式的交织视觉-语言trace表示（文本子目标+视觉关键帧交替）解决长期机器人操作中的语义-几何协同规划问题。在LIBERO和SimplerEnv基准上取得95.5%和59.4%的成功率，消融实验验证了双模态交织trace的必要性，但方法对全局规划错误的鲁棒性仍有局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉-语言-动作策略存在模态单一的问题：纯文本思维链虽有因果顺序但缺少空间约束；视觉预测虽提供几何线索但常局限于局部而语义约束不足。长期机器人操作任务需要同时满足逻辑一致性和几何落地性。

Method: 采用伪监督方法构建训练数据：时序分割演示 + 视觉语言模型为每个阶段生成描述。测试时，单个原生多模态Transformer从初始观测和指令自生成全局trace表示并缓存，作为闭环动作解码器的条件输入。

Result: LIBERO系列平均成功率达95.5%（含LIBERO-Long的92.4%），SimplerEnv-WidowX整体成功率为59.4%。消融实验表明trace表示对性能贡献显著，无trace时LIBERO-Long仅37.7%。抗干扰测试显示该方法对局部损坏和中等级别执行漂移有一定容忍度，但对全局规划错误或过期信息仍脆弱。

Conclusion: IVLR通过交替的文本子目标和视觉关键帧实现了长期任务规划中的语义-几何协同。消融实验证明双模态交织的trace表示是性能的关键：去掉trace后成功率从92.4%暴跌至37.7%，单独使用文本或视觉trace分别仅达62.0%和68.4%。但该方法对全局规划错误和过期信息的鲁棒性有限，仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Long-horizon robotic manipulation requires plans that are both logically coherent and geometrically grounded. Existing Vision-Language-Action policies usually hide planning in latent states or expose only one modality: text-only chain-of-thought encodes causal order but misses spatial constraints, while visual prediction provides geometric cues but often remains local and semantically underconstrained. We introduce Interleaved Vision--Language Reasoning (IVLR), a policy framework built around \trace{}, an explicit intermediate representation that alternates textual subgoals with visual keyframes over the full task horizon. At test time, a single native multimodal transformer self-generates this global semantic-geometric trace from the initial observation and instruction, caches it, and conditions a closed-loop action decoder on the trace, original instruction, and current observation. Because standard robot datasets lack such traces, we construct pseudo-supervision by temporally segmenting demonstrations and captioning each stage with a vision-language model. Across simulated benchmarks for long-horizon manipulation and visual distribution shift, \method{} reaches 95.5\% average success on LIBERO, including 92.4\% on LIBERO-Long, and 59.4\% overall success on SimplerEnv-WidowX. Ablations show that both modalities are necessary: without traces, LIBERO-Long success drops to 37.7\%; text-only and vision-only traces reach 62.0\% and 68.4\%, while the full interleaved trace reaches 92.4\%. Stress tests with execution perturbations and masked trace content show moderate degradation, suggesting that the trace can tolerate local corruption and moderate execution drift, but remains limited under stale or incorrect global plans.

</details>


### [164] [On the Role of Artificial Intelligence in Human-Machine Symbiosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00440)
*Ching-Chun Chang,Yuchen Guo,Hanrui Wang,Timo Spinde,Isao Echizen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: This paper addresses the challenge of tracing AI's functional role in natural language generation by proposing a methodology to infer, embed, and recover the latent role (assistive vs. creative) from generated text. The approach is validated through experiments and aims to support AI ethics considerations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: The boundary between human and AI-generated content is increasingly ambiguous. AI-generated information arises from mutual human-machine shaping rather than isolation. The key question shifts from whether AI participated to how it participated. When detached from dialogue context, the functional role of AI becomes difficult to trace or altogether unobservable.

Method: Proposed a methodology that infers latent AI roles from prompts, embeds these roles into the probabilistic generation process, and subsequently recovers the nature of AI participation from the resulting text. Evaluation conducted under two representative scenarios: AI as an assistive editor of human-written content, and AI as a creative generator from brief concepts.

Result: The experimental results validate the proposed methodology in terms of: (1) discrimination between different AI roles, (2) robustness against perturbations, and (3) preservation of linguistic quality.

Conclusion: This study presents a methodology to trace and recover AI's functional roles (assistive vs. creative) from generated text, validated through experiments, and contributes to future AI ethics research regarding fair, transparent, and appropriate AI usage.

Abstract: The evolution of artificial intelligence (AI) has rendered the boundary between humanity and computational machinery increasingly ambiguous. In the presence of more interwoven relationships within human-machine symbiosis, the very notion of AI-generated information becomes difficult to define, as such information arises not from either humans or machines in isolation, but from their mutual shaping. Therefore, a more pertinent question lies not merely in whether AI has participated, but in how it has participated. In general, the role assumed by AI is often specified, either implicitly or explicitly, in the input prompt, yet becomes less apparent or altogether unobservable when the generated content alone is available. Once detached from the dialogue context, the functional role may no longer be traceable. This study considers the problem of tracing the functional role played by AI in natural language generation. A methodology is proposed to infer the latent role specified by the prompt, embed this role into the content during the probabilistic generation process and subsequently recover the nature of AI participation from the resulting text. Experimentation is conducted under a representative scenario in which AI acts either as an assistive agent that edits human-written content or as a creative agent that generates new content from a brief concept. The experimental results support the validity of the proposed methodology in terms of discrimination between roles, robustness against perturbations and preservation of linguistic quality. We envision that this study may contribute to future research on the ethics of AI with regard to whether AI has been used fairly, transparently and appropriately.

</details>


### [165] [Instance-Aware Parameter Configuration in Bilevel Late Acceptance Hill Climbing for the Electric Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00572)
*Yinghao Qin,Xinwei Wang,Mosab Bazargani,Jun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对电动容量约束车辆路径问题(ECVRP)，提出了一种实例感知的参数配置方法。通过离线调优获取实例特定参数标签，并利用回归模型从实例特征预测参数。实验表明该方法相比全局调优配置在8个测试实例上平均目标值降低0.28%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组合优化中单一全局参数配置无法利用实例异质性，特别是在ECVRP中，不同实例在结构、需求模式和能源约束方面存在显著差异，因此需要实例感知的参数配置策略。

Method: 1) 离线调优程序获取实例特定参数标签；2) 从实例特征通过回归模型映射参数，实现对未知实例执行前的参数预测。

Result: 在IEEE WCCI 2020基准测试集及扩展集上，相比全局调优配置，该方法在8个保留测试实例上实现平均0.28%的目标值降低。

Conclusion: 实例感知参数配置能有效提升ECVRP求解性能，虽然改进幅度较小(0.28%)，但在大规模运输运营中具有显著的经济意义。

Abstract: Algorithm performance in combinatorial optimization is highly sensitive to parameter settings, while a single globally tuned configuration often fails to exploit the heterogeneity of instances. This limitation is particularly evident in the Electric Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem, where instances differ in structure, demand patterns, and energy constraints. This paper investigates instance-aware parameter configuration for Bilevel Late Acceptance Hill Climbing, a state-of-the-art metaheuristic for the Electric Capacitated Vehicle Routing Problem.
